    51	const androidNavigationBarColor = "#f7b967";

    58	function disableAutomaticFontScaling(Component) {
    59	  Component.defaultProps = Component.defaultProps || {};
    60	  Component.defaultProps.allowFontScaling = false;
    61	  Component.defaultProps.maxFontSizeMultiplier = 1;

    67	async function applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme({ dimStatusBar = false } = {}) {
    68	  if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;
    69	
    70	  try {
    71	    StatusBar.setHidden(false);
    72	    StatusBar.setTranslucent(false);
    73	    StatusBar.setBackgroundColor(
    74	      dimStatusBar ? androidStatusBarDimmedColor : androidNavigationBarColor,
    75	      true,
    76	    );
    77	    StatusBar.setBarStyle(androidStatusBarStyle, true);
    78	  } catch {}
    79	
    80	  try {
    81	    await NavigationBar.setVisibilityAsync("visible");
    82	  } catch {}
    83	
    84	  try {
    85	    await NavigationBar.setButtonStyleAsync(androidNavigationBarButtonStyle);
    86	  } catch {}
    87	}
    88	
    89	function AndroidNavigationBarTint({ dimStatusBar = false, pathname }) {
    90	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    91	
    92	  useEffect(() => {
    93	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") return undefined;
    94	
    95	    const applyTheme = () => applyAndroidNavigationBarTheme({ dimStatusBar });
    96	
    97	    applyTheme();
    98	    const restoreTimer = setTimeout(applyTheme, 250);
    99	    const finalRestoreTimer = setTimeout(applyTheme, 1000);
   100	    const appStateSubscription = AppState.addEventListener("change", (state) => {
   101	      if (state === "active") {
   102	        applyTheme();
   103	      }
   104	    });
   105	    let visibilitySubscription;
   106	
   107	    try {
   108	      visibilitySubscription = NavigationBar.addVisibilityListener(
   109	        applyTheme,
   110	      );
   111	    } catch {}
   112	
   113	    return () => {
   114	      clearTimeout(restoreTimer);
   115	      clearTimeout(finalRestoreTimer);
   116	      appStateSubscription.remove();
   117	      visibilitySubscription?.remove();
   118	    };
   119	  }, [dimStatusBar, pathname]);
   120	
   121	  if (Platform.OS !== "android" || insets.bottom <= 0) {
   122	    return null;
   123	  }
   124	
   125	  return (
   126	    <View
   127	      pointerEvents="none"
   128	      style={{
   129	        position: "absolute",
   130	        left: 0,
   131	        right: 0,
   132	        bottom: 0,
   133	        height: insets.bottom,
   134	        borderTopWidth: androidNavigationBarHairlineWidth,
   135	        borderTopColor: androidNavigationBarHairlineColor,
   136	        backgroundColor: androidNavigationBarColor,
   137	        zIndex: 1000001,
   138	        elevation: 1000001,
   139	      }}
   140	    />
   141	  );
   142	}
   143	
   144	function AndroidStatusBarTint({ dimmed = false }) {
   145	  const height =
   146	    Platform.OS === "android" && Number.isFinite(StatusBar.currentHeight)
   147	      ? Math.max(0, StatusBar.currentHeight)
   148	      : 0;
   149	
   150	  if (height <= 0) {
   151	    return null;
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  return (
   155	    <View
   156	      pointerEvents="none"
   157	      style={{
   158	        position: "absolute",
   159	        top: 0,
   160	        left: 0,
   161	        right: 0,
   162	        height,
   163	        backgroundColor: androidNavigationBarColor,
   164	        zIndex: 1000002,
   165	        elevation: 1000002,
   166	      }}
   167	    >
   168	      {dimmed ? (
   169	        <View
   170	          pointerEvents="none"
   171	          style={{
   172	            position: "absolute",
   173	            top: 0,
   174	            right: 0,
   175	            bottom: 0,
   176	            left: 0,
   177	            backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
   178	          }}
   179	        />
   180	      ) : null}
   181	    </View>
   182	  );
   183	}
   184	
   185	function RootLayoutContent({ headerScrollY }) {
   186	  const pathname = usePathname();
   187	  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
   188	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   189	  const { cartOverlayActionRequest, isShopOverlayVisible } = useShopCart();
   190	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   191	  const topSafeInset = getTopSafeInset(insets);
   192	
   193	  const showPersistentHeader = pathname !== "/shop";
   194	
   195	  const useOverlayHeader =
   196	    pathname === "/" ||
   197	    pathname === "/products" ||
   198	    pathname === "/aboutus" ||
   199	    pathname === "/contact";
   200	
   201	  const shouldProtectBackgroundHero =
   202	    useOverlayHeader &&
   203	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive || backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen);
   204	  const backgroundHeroLayerDepth = shouldProtectBackgroundHero ? 100 : 0;
   205	  const screenFadeTopOffset = pathname === "/shop"
   206	    ? 120 + topSafeInset
   207	    : 84 + topSafeInset;
   208	  const shouldShowScreenFade =
   209	    !(pathname === "/shop" && isShopOverlayVisible);
   210	  const shouldShowCartOpeningDim =
   211	    cartOverlayActionRequest.pending && pathname !== "/shop";
   212	  const shouldDimAndroidStatusBar =
   213	    Platform.OS === "android" && pathname === "/shop" && isShopOverlayVisible;
   214	
   215	  return (
   216	    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#FFFCF2" }}>
   217	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   218	        <View
   219	          pointerEvents="none"
   220	          style={{
   221	            position: "absolute",
   222	            top: 0,
   223	            left: 0,
   224	            right: 0,
   225	            zIndex: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   226	            elevation: backgroundHeroLayerDepth,
   227	          }}
   228	        >
   229	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showOnlyHero />
   230	        </View>
   231	      ) : null}
   232	
   233	      <MainScreenPushFrame>
   234	        <Stack
   235	          screenOptions={{
   236	            headerShown: false,
   237	            animation: "none",
   238	            contentStyle: {
   239	              backgroundColor: useOverlayHeader
   240	                ? "transparent"
   241	                : "#FFFCF2",
   242	            },
   243	          }}
   244	        />
   245	      </MainScreenPushFrame>
   246	
   247	      {shouldShowScreenFade ? (
   248	        <ScreenFade
   249	          showTopFade={!useOverlayHeader}
   250	          topOffset={screenFadeTopOffset}
   251	        />
   252	      ) : null}
   253	
   254	      {showPersistentHeader && useOverlayHeader ? (
   255	        <View
   256	          pointerEvents="box-none"
   257	          style={{
   258	            position: "absolute",
   259	            top: 0,
   260	            left: 0,
   261	            right: 0,
   262	            zIndex: 1000000,
   263	            elevation: 1000000,
   264	          }}
   265	        >
   266	          <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} showHero={false} />
   267	        </View>
   268	      ) : null}
   269	
   270	      {showPersistentHeader && !useOverlayHeader ? (
   271	        <AppHeader scrollY={headerScrollY} />
   272	      ) : null}
   273	
   274	      {shouldShowCartOpeningDim ? (
   275	        <View
   276	          pointerEvents="none"
   277	          style={{
   278	            position: "absolute",
   279	            top: 0,
   280	            right: 0,
   281	            bottom: 0,
   282	            left: 0,
   283	            backgroundColor: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
   284	            zIndex: 1000001,
   285	            elevation: 1000001,
   286	          }}
   287	        />
   288	      ) : null}
   289	
   290	      <StickyCartButton />
   291	
   292	      <AndroidStatusBarTint
   293	        dimmed={shouldDimAndroidStatusBar}
   294	      />
   295	      <AndroidNavigationBarTint
   296	        dimStatusBar={shouldDimAndroidStatusBar}
   297	        pathname={pathname}
   298	      />
   299	    </View>
   300	  );
   301	}
   302	
   303	export default function RootLayout() {
   304	  const headerScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   305	
   306	  const [fontsLoaded] = useFonts({
   307	    "Dream Avenue": require("../assets/fonts/dream_avenue/dream_avenue.ttf"),
   308	    "Great Vibes": require("../assets/fonts/great_vibes/GreatVibes-Regular.ttf"),
   309	    "TT Fors": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_regular.ttf"),
   310	    "TT Fors Demibold": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_demibold.ttf"),
   311	    "TT Fors Light": require("../assets/fonts/tt_fors/tt_fors_trial_light.ttf"),
   312	  });
   313	
   314	  if (!fontsLoaded) {
   315	    return null;
   316	  }
   317	
   318	  return (
   319	    <SafeAreaProvider>
   320	      <StripeProvider
   321	        merchantIdentifier={stripeMerchantIdentifier || undefined}
   322	        publishableKey={stripePublishableKey}
   323	        setReturnUrlSchemeOnAndroid
   324	        urlScheme={stripeUrlScheme}
   325	      >
   326	        <ThemeProvider value={navigationTheme}>
   327	          <HeaderScrollProvider scrollY={headerScrollY}>
   328	            <BackgroundHeroStateProvider sourceScrollY={headerScrollY}>
   329	              <HeaderSwipeProvider>
   330	                <ShopCartProvider>
   331	                  <RootLayoutContent headerScrollY={headerScrollY} />
   332	                </ShopCartProvider>
   333	              </HeaderSwipeProvider>
   334	            </BackgroundHeroStateProvider>
   335	          </HeaderScrollProvider>
   336	        </ThemeProvider>
   337	      </StripeProvider>
   338	    </SafeAreaProvider>
   339	  );
   340	}

     1	import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     2	import {
     3	  Animated,
     4	  Image,
     5	  StyleSheet,
     6	  Text,
     7	  View,
     8	  useWindowDimensions,
     9	} from "react-native";

    11

    13	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
    14	import PageDivider from "../components/PageDivider";
    15	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
    16	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
    17	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    18	import {
    19	  getFeatureImageWidth,
    20	  scaleLineHeight,
    21	} from "../utils/responsiveLayout";
    22	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    23	
    24	const passionImageBlendScrollDistance = 720;
    25	const passionImageBlendFeatherHeight = 170;
    26	const passionImageBlendOverlayColor = "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.92)";
    27	const featureImageAspectRatio = 1280 / 853;
    28	const homeFeatureMagnifiedScale = 1.08;
    29	const homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio = 0.5;
    30	
    31	function isMatchingLayout(currentLayout, nextLayout) {
    32	  return (
    33	    currentLayout &&
    34	    Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
    35	    Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
    36	  );
    37	}
    38	
    39	export default function HomeScreen() {
    40	  const [imageLayouts, setImageLayouts] = useState({});
    41	  const [paragraphLayouts, setParagraphLayouts] = useState({});
    42	  const {
    43	    compactTopLayout,
    44	    initialContentOffset,
    45	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    46	    scrollHandlers,
    47	    scrollY,
    48	  } =
    49	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    50	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    51	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    52	  const croppedImageWidth = getFeatureImageWidth(windowWidth);
    53	  const croppedImageHeight = croppedImageWidth * featureImageAspectRatio;
    54	  const passionImageBlendTranslateY = scrollY.interpolate({
    55	    inputRange: [0, passionImageBlendScrollDistance],
    56	    outputRange: [-passionImageBlendFeatherHeight, croppedImageHeight],
    57	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    58	  });
    59	  const handleParagraphLayout = useCallback(
    60	    (featureName) =>
    61	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    62	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    63	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    64	
    65	        setParagraphLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    66	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    67	            return currentLayouts;
    68	          }
    69	
    70	          return {
    71	            ...currentLayouts,
    72	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    73	          };
    74	        });
    75	      },
    76	    []
    77	  );
    78	  const handleImageLayout = useCallback(
    79	    (featureName) =>
    80	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    81	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    82	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    83	
    84	        setImageLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    85	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[featureName], nextLayout)) {
    86	            return currentLayouts;
    87	          }
    88	
    89	          return {
    90	            ...currentLayouts,
    91	            [featureName]: nextLayout,
    92	          };
    93	        });
    94	      },
    95	    []
    96	  );
    97	
    98	  return (
    99	    <View style={sharedStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   100	      <Animated.ScrollView
   101	        style={sharedStyles.scroll}
   102	        contentContainerStyle={[
   103	          sharedStyles.scrollContent,
   104	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   105	        ]}
   106	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   107	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   108	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   109	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   110	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   111	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   112	        {...scrollHandlers}
   113	      >
   114	        <View style={sharedStyles.main}>
   115	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   116	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   117	            pageTitleStyle={[sharedStyles.pageTitle, homeStyles.pageTitle]}
   118	            scrollY={scrollY}
   119	          />
   120	
   121	          <CenterMagnifyView
   122	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.passion}
   123	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.passion}
   124	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   125	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   126	            scrollY={scrollY}
   127	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   128	          >
   129	            <View
   130	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("passion")}
   131	              style={[
   132	                sharedStyles.directionalImageBlendWrap,
   133	                { width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight },
   134	              ]}
   135	            >
   136	              <Image
   137	                pointerEvents="none"
   138	                source={require("../passion111_mos9_bright_italian_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   139	                style={[
   140	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   141	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   142	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   143	                ]}
   144	                resizeMode="contain"
   145	              />
   146	              <Animated.View
   147	                pointerEvents="none"
   148	                style={[
   149	                  sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendPanel,
   150	                  {
   151	                    height:
   152	                      croppedImageHeight + passionImageBlendFeatherHeight,
   153	                    transform: [{ translateY: passionImageBlendTranslateY }],
   154	                  },
   155	                ]}
   156	              >
   157	                <LinearGradient
   158	                  colors={[
   159	                    "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
   160	                    passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   161	                  ]}
   162	                  locations={[0, 1]}
   163	                  style={sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendFeather}
   164	                />
   165	                <View
   166	                  style={[
   167	                    sharedStyles.topToBottomImageBlendSolid,
   168	                    {
   169	                      height: croppedImageHeight,
   170	                      backgroundColor: passionImageBlendOverlayColor,
   171	                    },
   172	                  ]}
   173	                />
   174	              </Animated.View>
   175	            </View>
   176	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   177	              Passion
   178	            </Text>
   179	            <Text
   180	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("passion")}
   181	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   182	            >
   183	              Every Alla Vostra board is prepared with care, precision, and a
   184	              love for the grazing experience.
   185	            </Text>
   186	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   187	
   188	          <PageDivider />
   189	
   190	          <CenterMagnifyView
   191	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.taste}
   192	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.taste}
   193	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   194	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   195	            scrollY={scrollY}
   196	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   197	          >
   198	            <View
   199	              onLayout={handleImageLayout("taste")}
   200	              style={{ width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight }}
   201	            >
   202	              <Image
   203	                pointerEvents="none"
   204	                source={require("../taste111_mos9_bright_soft_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   205	                style={[
   206	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   207	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   208	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   209	                ]}
   210	                resizeMode="contain"
   211	              />
   212	            </View>
   213	            <Text style={[sharedStyles.featureTitle, homeStyles.featureTitle]}>
   214	              Taste
   215	            </Text>
   216	            <Text
   217	              onLayout={handleParagraphLayout("taste")}
   218	              style={[sharedStyles.featureText, homeStyles.featureText]}
   219	            >
   220	              Our boards are built around layered flavor: cheeses, meats,
   221	              fruits, sweets, spreads, and accoutrements.
   222	            </Text>
   223	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   224	
   225	          <PageDivider />
   226	
   227	          <CenterMagnifyView
   228	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayouts.convenience}
   229	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={paragraphLayouts.convenience}
   230	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={homeFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   231	            magnifiedScale={homeFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   232	            scrollY={scrollY}
   233	            style={sharedStyles.featureBlock}
   234	          >

   238	            >

   241	                source={require("../convenience111_mos9_bright_soft_mockup_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   242	                style={[
   243	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   244	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },

   247	                resizeMode="contain"

   259	            </Text>

   261	        </View>

   110	
   111	function ProductSection({
   112	  croppedImageWidth,
   113	  onBuyPress,
   114	  onBuyButtonLayout,
   115	  onImageLayout,
   116	  onLowerContentLayout,
   117	  product,
   118	}) {
   119	  return (
   120	    <View style={productsStyles.productCard}>
   121	      <View onLayout={onImageLayout} style={{ width: croppedImageWidth }}>
   122	        <View style={productsStyles.productImageWrap}>
   123	          <Image
   124	            pointerEvents="none"
   125	            source={product.image}
   126	            style={[
   127	              productsStyles.productImage,
   128	              { height: croppedImageWidth },
   129	            ]}
   130	            resizeMode="contain"
   131	            accessibilityLabel={product.alt}
   132	          />
   133	        </View>
   134	      </View>
   135	
   136	      <View
   137	        onLayout={onLowerContentLayout}
   138	        style={productsStyles.productLowerContent}
   139	      >
   140	        <Text style={productsStyles.productTitle}>{product.title}</Text>
   141	        <Text style={productsStyles.productDescription}>{product.serving}</Text>
   142	
   143	        <View style={productsStyles.productDetails}>
   144	          {product.includes.map((section) => (
   145	            <View key={section.title} style={productsStyles.includeSection}>
   146	              <View style={productsStyles.includeHeaderRow}>
   147	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeTitle}>{section.title}</Text>
   148	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeAmount}>{section.amount}</Text>
   149	              </View>
   150	
   151	              {section.items.map((item) => (
   152	                <View key={item} style={productsStyles.includeItemRow}>
   153	                  <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleBorder}>
   154	                    <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangleOuter} />
   155	                    <View style={productsStyles.includeItemTriangle} />
   156	                  </View>
   157	                  <Text style={productsStyles.includeItem}>{item}</Text>
   158	                </View>
   159	              ))}
   160	
   161	              {section.note ? (
   162	                <Text style={productsStyles.includeNote}>{section.note}</Text>
   163	              ) : null}
   164	            </View>
   165	          ))}
   166	        </View>
   167	
   168	        <Pressable
   169	          accessibilityLabel={`Buy ${product.title}`}
   170	          accessibilityRole="button"
   171	          onLayout={onBuyButtonLayout}
   172	          onPress={onBuyPress}
   173	          style={({ pressed }) => [
   174	            productsStyles.buyButton,
   175	            pressed && productsStyles.buyButtonPressed,
   176	          ]}
   177	        >
   178	          <ProductOrangeButtonGradient />
   179	          <Text
   180	            adjustsFontSizeToFit
   181	            allowFontScaling={false}
   182	            minimumFontScale={0.72}
   183	            numberOfLines={1}
   184	            style={productsStyles.buyButtonText}
   185	          >
   186	            {`Buy ${product.title}`}
   187	          </Text>
   188	        </Pressable>
   189	      </View>
   190	    </View>
   191	  );
   192	}
   193	
   194	function ProductMagnifySection({ croppedImageWidth, onBuyPress, product, scrollY }) {
   195	  const [buyButtonLayout, setBuyButtonLayout] = useState(null);
   196	  const [imageLayout, setImageLayout] = useState(null);
   197	  const [lowerContentLayout, setLowerContentLayout] = useState(null);
   198	
   199	  const updateLayout = useCallback((setter) => ({ nativeEvent }) => {
   200	    const nextLayout = nativeEvent.layout;
   201	
   202	    setter((currentLayout) => {
   203	      if (
   204	        currentLayout &&
   205	        Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
   206	        Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
   207	      ) {
   208	        return currentLayout;
   209	      }
   210	
   211	      return nextLayout;
   212	    });
   213	  }, []);
   214	
   215	  return (
   216	    <CenterMagnifyView
   217	      magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={imageLayout}
   218	      magnifyExitAnchorLayout={buyButtonLayout || lowerContentLayout}
   219	      scrollY={scrollY}
   220	      style={productsStyles.productSectionWrap}
   221	    >
   222	      <ProductSection
   223	        croppedImageWidth={croppedImageWidth}
   224	        onBuyButtonLayout={updateLayout(setBuyButtonLayout)}
   225	        onBuyPress={onBuyPress}
   226	        onImageLayout={updateLayout(setImageLayout)}
   227	        onLowerContentLayout={updateLayout(setLowerContentLayout)}
   228	        product={product}
   229	      />
   230	    </CenterMagnifyView>
   231	  );
   232	}
   233	
   234	export default function ProductsScreen() {
   235	  const {
   236	    compactTopLayout,
   237	    initialContentOffset,
   238	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
   239	    scrollHandlers,
   240	    scrollY,
   241	  } =
   242	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
   243	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
   244	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   245	  const croppedImageWidth = getFeatureImageWidth(windowWidth);
   246	  const openProductInShop = (productTitle) => {
   247	    router.push({
   248	      pathname: "/shop",
   249	      params: {
   250	        openProduct: String(Date.now()),
   251	        product: productTitle,
   252	      },
   253	    });
   254	  };
   255	
   256	  return (
   257	    <View style={productsStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   258	      <Animated.ScrollView
   259	        style={productsStyles.scroll}
   260	        contentContainerStyle={[
   261	          productsStyles.scrollContent,
   262	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   263	        ]}
   264	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   265	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   266	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   267	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   268	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   269	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   270	        {...scrollHandlers}
   271	      >
   272	        <View style={productsStyles.main}>
   273	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   274	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   275	            pageTitleStyle={productsStyles.pageTitle}
   276	            scrollY={scrollY}
   277	          />
   278	
   279	          {products.map((product, index) => (
   280	            <Fragment key={product.title}>
   281	              <ProductMagnifySection
   282	                croppedImageWidth={croppedImageWidth}
   283	                onBuyPress={() => openProductInShop(product.title)}
   284	                product={product}
   285	                scrollY={scrollY}
   286	              />
   287	              {index < products.length - 1 ? <PageDivider /> : null}
   288	            </Fragment>
   289	          ))}
   290	        </View>
   291	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   292	    </View>
   293	  );
   294	}

FILE: mob/app/aboutus.js

     1	import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     2	import { Animated, Image, Text, View, useWindowDimensions } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
     5	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
     6	import aboutusStyles from "../styles/aboutusStyles";
     7	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
     8	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
     9	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    10	import { getFeatureImageWidth } from "../utils/responsiveLayout";
    11	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    12	
    13	const featureImageAspectRatio = 1280 / 853;
    14	const aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale = 1.08;
    15	const aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio = 0.5;
    16	
    17	function isMatchingLayout(currentLayout, nextLayout) {
    18	  return (
    19	    currentLayout &&
    20	    Math.abs(currentLayout.y - nextLayout.y) < 0.5 &&
    21	    Math.abs(currentLayout.height - nextLayout.height) < 0.5
    22	  );
    23	}
    24	
    25	export default function AboutusScreen() {
    26	  const [sectionLayouts, setSectionLayouts] = useState({});
    27	  const {
    28	    compactTopLayout,
    29	    initialContentOffset,
    30	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    31	    scrollHandlers,
    32	    scrollY,
    33	  } =
    34	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    35	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    36	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
    37	  const croppedImageWidth = getFeatureImageWidth(windowWidth);
    38	  const croppedImageHeight = croppedImageWidth * featureImageAspectRatio;
    39	  const handleSectionLayout = useCallback(
    40	    (sectionName) =>
    41	      ({ nativeEvent }) => {
    42	        const { height, y } = nativeEvent.layout;
    43	        const nextLayout = { height, y };
    44	
    45	        setSectionLayouts((currentLayouts) => {
    46	          if (isMatchingLayout(currentLayouts[sectionName], nextLayout)) {
    47	            return currentLayouts;
    48	          }
    49	
    50	          return {
    51	            ...currentLayouts,
    52	            [sectionName]: nextLayout,
    53	          };
    54	        });
    55	      },
    56	    []
    57	  );
    58	
    59	  return (
    60	    <View style={aboutusStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
    61	      <Animated.ScrollView
    62	        style={aboutusStyles.scroll}
    63	        contentContainerStyle={[
    64	          aboutusStyles.scrollContent,
    65	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
    66	        ]}
    67	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
    68	        decelerationRate={0.95}
    69	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
    70	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
    71	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
    72	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
    73	        {...scrollHandlers}
    74	      >
    75	        <View style={aboutusStyles.main}>
    76	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
    77	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
    78	            pageTitleStyle={aboutusStyles.pageTitle}
    79	            scrollY={scrollY}
    80	          />
    81	
    82	          <CenterMagnifyView
    83	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.image}
    84	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.image}
    85	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
    86	            magnifiedScale={aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale}
    87	            scrollY={scrollY}
    88	            style={aboutusStyles.aboutSection}
    89	          >
    90	            <View
    91	              onLayout={handleSectionLayout("image")}
    92	              style={[
    93	                aboutusStyles.imageWrap,
    94	                { width: croppedImageWidth, height: croppedImageHeight },
    95	              ]}
    96	            >
    97	              <Image
    98	                pointerEvents="none"
    99	                source={require("../convenience2_content_fill_mockup_1_plain_gray_gradient_mos9_tile_blend_both_mockup.png")}
   100	                style={[
   101	                  sharedStyles.featureImage,
   102	                  { backgroundColor: "transparent" },
   103	                  { height: croppedImageHeight },
   104	                ]}
   105	                resizeMode="contain"
   106	              />
   107	            </View>
   108	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   109	
   110	          <CenterMagnifyView
   111	            magnifyEnterAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph1}
   112	            magnifyExitAnchorLayout={sectionLayouts.paragraph1}
   113	            magnifyRampViewportRatio={aboutFeatureMagnifyRampViewportRatio}
   114	            magnifiedScale={aboutFeatureMagnifiedScale}
   115	            scrollY={scrollY}
   116	            style={[aboutusStyles.aboutSection, aboutusStyles.copy]}
   117	          >
   118	            <Text
   119	              onLayout={handleSectionLayout("paragraph1")}
   120	              style={[
   121	                aboutusStyles.paragraph,
   122	                aboutusStyles.paragraphSectionGap,
   123	              ]}
   124	            >
   125	              Since we served up our very first cheeseplate, Alla Vostra has
   126	              been about one thing and one thing only: providing our customers
   127	              with the most intricate and delicious grazing boards you'll find
   128	              anywhere in the Miami metropolitan area.
   129	            </Text>
   130	          </CenterMagnifyView>

   139	          >

============================================================
FILE: mob/app/contact.js
============================================================

     1	import { useState } from "react";
     2	import { Alert, Animated, Text, TextInput, View } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	import CenterMagnifyView from "../components/CenterMagnifyView";
     5	import HapticPressable from "../components/HapticPressable";
     6	import MainScreenIntroSpacer from "../components/MainScreenIntroSpacer";
     7	import contactStyles from "../styles/contactStyles";
     8	import { sendContactMessage } from "../utils/contactMessages";
     9	import { useMainScreenScrollProps } from "../utils/mainScreenScrollContext";
    10	import { getMainScreenScrollViewProps } from "../utils/platformLayout";
    11	import useMainScreenSwipeNavigation from "../utils/useMainScreenSwipeNavigation";
    12	
    13	export default function ContactScreen() {
    14	  const [name, setName] = useState("");
    15	  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
    16	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
    17	  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
    18	  const [emptyTouchedFieldKeys, setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys] = useState({});
    19	  const [blurredFieldKeys, setBlurredFieldKeys] = useState({});
    20	  const [isSending, setIsSending] = useState(false);
    21	  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState("");
    22	  const {
    23	    compactTopLayout,
    24	    initialContentOffset,
    25	    scrollContentInsetStyle,
    26	    scrollHandlers,
    27	    scrollY,
    28	  } =
    29	    useMainScreenScrollProps();
    30	  const screenSwipeHandlers = useMainScreenSwipeNavigation();
    31	  const trimmedName = name.trim();
    32	  const trimmedEmail = email.trim();
    33	  const trimmedMessage = message.trim();
    34	  const isEmailValid = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(trimmedEmail);
    35	  const canSubmit =
    36	    trimmedName.length > 0 &&
    37	    isEmailValid &&
    38	    trimmedMessage.length > 0 &&
    39	    !isSending;
    40	
    41	  const contactFieldValues = {
    42	    email,
    43	    message,
    44	    name,
    45	    phone,
    46	  };
    47	  const shouldShowFieldFault = (fieldKey) =>
    48	    Boolean(emptyTouchedFieldKeys[fieldKey]) ||
    49	    (fieldKey === "email" &&
    50	      Boolean(blurredFieldKeys.email) &&
    51	      trimmedEmail.length > 0 &&
    52	      !isEmailValid);
    53	
    54	  const clearFieldFault = (fieldKey) => {
    55	    setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    56	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    57	        return currentFieldKeys;
    58	      }
    59	
    60	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    61	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    62	      return nextFieldKeys;
    63	    });
    64	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    65	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    66	        return currentFieldKeys;
    67	      }
    68	
    69	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    70	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    71	      return nextFieldKeys;
    72	    });
    73	  };
    74	
    75	  const markFieldBlurred = (fieldKey) => {
    76	    const fieldValue = contactFieldValues[fieldKey] || "";
    77	
    78	    setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
    79	      ...currentFieldKeys,
    80	      [fieldKey]: true,
    81	    }));
    82	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
    83	      const isFieldEmpty = fieldValue.trim().length === 0;
    84	
    85	      if (isFieldEmpty) {
    86	        return {
    87	          ...currentFieldKeys,
    88	          [fieldKey]: true,
    89	        };
    90	      }
    91	
    92	      if (!currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
    93	        return currentFieldKeys;
    94	      }
    95	
    96	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
    97	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
    98	      return nextFieldKeys;
    99	    });
   100	  };
   101	
   102	  const updateContactField = (fieldKey, text, setter) => {
   103	    setter(text);
   104	    setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
   105	      if (text.trim().length === 0 || !currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
   106	        return currentFieldKeys;
   107	      }
   108	
   109	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
   110	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
   111	      return nextFieldKeys;
   112	    });
   113	  };
   114	
   115	  const showContactAlert = (title, alertMessage) => {
   116	    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.alert === "function") {
   117	      window.alert(`${title}\n${alertMessage}`);
   118	      return;
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    Alert.alert(title, alertMessage);
   122	  };
   123	
   124	  const handleSubmit = async () => {
   125	    if (!canSubmit) {
   126	      setBlurredFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
   127	        ...currentFieldKeys,
   128	        email: true,
   129	      }));
   130	      setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => ({
   131	        ...currentFieldKeys,
   132	        ...(trimmedName.length === 0 ? { name: true } : {}),
   133	        ...(trimmedEmail.length === 0 ? { email: true } : {}),
   134	        ...(trimmedMessage.length === 0 ? { message: true } : {}),
   135	      }));
   136	      setStatusMessage("Please enter your name, email, and message.");
   137	      return;
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    setIsSending(true);
   141	    setStatusMessage("");
   142	
   143	    try {
   144	      await sendContactMessage({
   145	        email: trimmedEmail,
   146	        message: trimmedMessage,
   147	        name: trimmedName,
   148	        phone: phone.trim(),
   149	      });
   150	      setName("");
   151	      setEmail("");
   152	      setPhone("");
   153	      setMessage("");
   154	      setBlurredFieldKeys({});
   155	      setEmptyTouchedFieldKeys({});
   156	      setStatusMessage("Message sent. We will respond within 48 hours.");
   157	      showContactAlert(
   158	        "Message sent",
   159	        "Thank you. Your message was sent to Alla Vostra.",
   160	      );
   161	    } catch (error) {
   162	      const errorMessage = error?.message || "Please try again.";
   163	      setStatusMessage(errorMessage);
   164	      showContactAlert("Message not sent", errorMessage);
   165	    } finally {
   166	      setIsSending(false);
   167	    }
   168	  };
   169	
   170	  return (
   171	    <View style={contactStyles.screen} {...screenSwipeHandlers}>
   172	      <Animated.ScrollView
   173	        style={contactStyles.scroll}
   174	        contentContainerStyle={[
   175	          contactStyles.scrollContent,
   176	          scrollContentInsetStyle,
   177	        ]}
   178	        contentOffset={initialContentOffset}
   179	        decelerationRate={0.95}
   180	        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
   181	        scrollEventThrottle={16}
   182	        {...getMainScreenScrollViewProps()}
   183	        {...screenSwipeHandlers}
   184	        {...scrollHandlers}
   185	      >
   186	        <View style={contactStyles.main}>
   187	          <MainScreenIntroSpacer
   188	            compactTopLayout={compactTopLayout}
   189	            pageTitleStyle={contactStyles.pageTitle}
   190	            scrollY={scrollY}
   191	          />
   192	
   193	          <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   194	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>Got a business inquiry?</Text>
   195	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>
   196	              General questions about our products?
   197	            </Text>
   198	            <Text style={contactStyles.introText}>
   199	              We strive to respond within 48 hours.
   200	            </Text>
   201	          </CenterMagnifyView>
   202	
   203	          <View style={contactStyles.formCard}>
   204	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   205	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Full Name</Text>
   206	              <TextInput
   207	                style={[
   208	                  contactStyles.input,
   209	                  shouldShowFieldFault("name") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   210	                ]}
   211	                placeholder="Your Name"
   212	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   213	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("name")}
   214	                onChangeText={(text) => updateContactField("name", text, setName)}
   215	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("name")}
   216	                returnKeyType="next"
   217	                textContentType="name"
   218	                value={name}
   219	              />
   220	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   221	
   222	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   223	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Email Address</Text>
   224	              <TextInput
   225	                style={[
   226	                  contactStyles.input,
   227	                  shouldShowFieldFault("email") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   228	                ]}
   229	                placeholder="you@company.com"
   230	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   231	                keyboardType="email-address"
   232	                autoCapitalize="none"
   233	                autoCorrect={false}
   234	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("email")}
   235	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   236	                  updateContactField("email", text, setEmail)
   237	                }
   238	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("email")}
   239	                returnKeyType="next"
   240	                textContentType="emailAddress"
   241	                value={email}
   242	              />
   243	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   244	
   245	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   246	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Phone Number</Text>
   247	              <TextInput
   248	                style={[
   249	                  contactStyles.input,
   250	                  shouldShowFieldFault("phone") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   251	                ]}
   252	                placeholder="+1 (555) 1234-567"
   253	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   254	                keyboardType="phone-pad"
   255	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("phone")}
   256	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   257	                  updateContactField("phone", text, setPhone)
   258	                }
   259	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("phone")}
   260	                returnKeyType="next"
   261	                textContentType="telephoneNumber"
   262	                value={phone}
   263	              />
   264	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   265	
   266	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY}>
   267	              <Text style={contactStyles.label}>Your Message</Text>
   268	              <TextInput
   269	                style={[
   270	                  contactStyles.input,
   271	                  contactStyles.messageInput,
   272	                  shouldShowFieldFault("message") && contactStyles.inputFaulty,
   273	                ]}
   274	                placeholder="Your Message"
   275	                placeholderTextColor="rgba(17, 17, 17, 0.38)"
   276	                multiline
   277	                onBlur={() => markFieldBlurred("message")}
   278	                onChangeText={(text) =>
   279	                  updateContactField("message", text, setMessage)
   280	                }
   281	                onFocus={() => clearFieldFault("message")}
   282	                returnKeyType="default"
   283	                textAlignVertical="top"
   284	                value={message}
   285	              />
   286	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   287	
   288	            <CenterMagnifyView scrollY={scrollY} style={contactStyles.buttonWrap}>
   289	              <HapticPressable
   290	                accessibilityRole="button"
   291	                disabled={isSending}
   292	                onPress={handleSubmit}
   293	                style={({ pressed }) => [
   294	                  contactStyles.button,
   295	                  (!canSubmit || pressed) && contactStyles.buttonDimmed,
   296	                ]}
   297	              >
   298	                <Text style={contactStyles.buttonText}>
   299	                  {isSending ? "Sending..." : "Send Message"}
   300	                </Text>
   301	              </HapticPressable>
   302	              {statusMessage ? (
   303	                <Text style={contactStyles.statusText}>{statusMessage}</Text>
   304	              ) : null}
   305	            </CenterMagnifyView>
   306	          </View>
   307	        </View>
   308	      </Animated.ScrollView>
   309	    </View>
   310	  );
   311	}

   600	
   601	  if (lastTwoDigits >= 11 && lastTwoDigits <= 13) {
   602	    return `${dateNumber}th`;
   603	  }
   604	
   605	  switch (dateNumber % 10) {
   606	    case 1:
   607	      return `${dateNumber}st`;
   608	    case 2:
   609	      return `${dateNumber}nd`;
   610	    case 3:
   611	      return `${dateNumber}rd`;
   612	    default:
   613	      return `${dateNumber}th`;
   614	  }

   679	function getStripeCardBrandLabel(brand) {
   680	  switch (brand) {
   681	    case "Visa":
   682	      return "VISA";
   683	    case "MasterCard":
   684	      return "MASTERCARD";
   685	    case "AmericanExpress":
   686	      return "AMEX";
   687	    case "Unknown":
   688	    case undefined:
   689	    case null:
   690	      return "";
   691	    default:
   692	      return String(brand)
   693	        .replace(/([a-z])([A-Z])/g, "$1 $2")
   694	        .toUpperCase();
   695	  }

   900	function PiccolaQuantityTriangle({ direction, muted }) {
   901	  const strokeInset = piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth / 2;
   902	  const strokeColor = muted ? "#888888" : "#111111";
   903	  const isUpTriangle = direction === "up";
   904	  const fillGradientId = isUpTriangle
   905	    ? "piccolaQuantityTriangleTopGradient"
   906	    : "piccolaQuantityTriangleBottomGradient";
   907	  const fillGradientColors = isUpTriangle
   908	    ? topOverlayGradientColors
   909	    : orangeButtonGradientColors;
   910	  const path =
   911	    isUpTriangle
   912	      ? [
   913	          `M ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${strokeInset}`,
   914	          `L ${
   915	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   916	          } ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset}`,
   917	          `L ${strokeInset} ${
   918	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   919	          }`,
   920	          "Z",
   921	        ].join(" ")
   922	      : [
   923	          `M ${strokeInset} ${strokeInset}`,
   924	          `L ${
   925	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth - strokeInset
   926	          } ${strokeInset}`,
   927	          `L ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2} ${
   928	            piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight - strokeInset
   929	          }`,
   930	          "Z",
   931	        ].join(" ");
   932	
   933	  return (
   934	    <Svg
   935	      height="100%"
   936	      style={shopStyles.piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleSvg}
   937	      viewBox={`0 0 ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth} ${piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight}`}
   938	      width="100%"
   939	    >
   940	      <Defs>
   941	        <SvgLinearGradient
   942	          gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"
   943	          id={fillGradientId}
   944	          x1={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2}
   945	          x2={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleWidth / 2}
   946	          y1={isUpTriangle ? piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight : 0}
   947	          y2={isUpTriangle ? 0 : piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleHeight}
   948	        >
   949	          {fillGradientColors.map((color, index) => (
   950	            <Stop
   951	              key={`${fillGradientId}-${color}`}
   952	              offset={`${index * 50}%`}
   953	              stopColor={color}
   954	            />
   955	          ))}
   956	        </SvgLinearGradient>
   957	      </Defs>
   958	      <Path
   959	        d={path}
   960	        fill={`url(#${fillGradientId})`}
   961	        opacity={muted ? 0.5 : 1}
   962	        stroke={strokeColor}
   963	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   964	        strokeWidth={piccolaOverlayQuantityTriangleStrokeWidth}
   965	      />
   966	    </Svg>
   967	  );
   968	}

  1612	          ...currentFieldKeys,
  1613	          [fieldKey]: true,

  1618	        return currentFieldKeys;
  1619	      }
  1620	
  1621	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
  1622	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];

  1703	  const shakeDeliveryOverlay = () => {

  1717	        duration: 45,
  1718	        easing: Easing.linear,
  1719	        toValue: 8,
  1720	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1721	      }),
  1722	      Animated.timing(deliveryOverlayShakeX, {
  1723	        duration: 45,
  1724	        easing: Easing.linear,
  1725	        toValue: -6,
  1726	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1727	      }),
  1728	      Animated.timing(deliveryOverlayShakeX, {
  1729	        duration: 45,
  1730	        easing: Easing.linear,
  1731	        toValue: 6,
  1732	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1733	      }),
  1734	      Animated.timing(deliveryOverlayShakeX, {
  1735	        duration: 45,
  1736	        easing: Easing.out(Easing.quad),
  1737	        toValue: 0,
  1738	        useNativeDriver: true,
  1739	      }),
  1740	    ]);
  1741	    deliveryOverlayShakeAnimationRef.current.start(({ finished }) => {
  1742	      if (finished) {
  1743	        deliveryOverlayShakeAnimationRef.current = null;
  1744	      }
  1745	    });
  1746	  };
  1747	
  1748	  const selectDeliveryStateOption = (option) => {
  1749	    setSelectedDeliveryState(option);
  1750	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(null);
  1751	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  1752

  1754	      shakeDeliveryOverlay();
  1755	    }
  1756	  };

  1758	  const selectDeliveryCityOption = (option) => {

  1771	  const updateDeliveryFieldValue = (fieldKey, text) => {
  1772	    const nextValue = fieldKey === "zip" ? normalizeDeliveryZip(text) : text;
  1773	
  1774	    setDeliveryFieldValues((currentValues) => ({
  1775	      ...currentValues,
  1776	      [fieldKey]: nextValue,
  1777	    }));
  1778	    setEmptyTouchedDeliveryFieldKeys((currentFieldKeys) => {
  1779	      if (nextValue.trim().length === 0 || !currentFieldKeys[fieldKey]) {
  1780	        return currentFieldKeys;
  1781	      }
  1782	
  1783	      const nextFieldKeys = { ...currentFieldKeys };
  1784	      delete nextFieldKeys[fieldKey];
  1785	      return nextFieldKeys;
  1786	    });
  1787	
  1788	    if (
  1789	      fieldKey === "zip" &&
  1790	      nextValue.length === 5 &&
  1791	      !isDeliveryServiceAreaZip(nextValue)
  1792	    ) {
  1793	      shakeDeliveryOverlay();

  1797	  const toggleDeliveryTimeDropdown = (fieldKey) => {

  1800	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  1801	    setIsPaymentIssuerDropdownOpen(false);

  1822	
  1823	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex = (options, optionIndex) =>
  1824	    deliveryTimeWheelLoopMidpoint * options.length + optionIndex;
  1825	
  1826	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY = (scrollY) =>
  1827	    Math.max(0, Math.round(scrollY / deliveryTimeWheelScrollStepHeight));
  1828	
  1829	  const getDeliveryTimeWheelOptionIndexFromScrollY = (options, scrollY) => {
  1830	    if (options.length === 0) {
  1831	      return 0;
  1832	    }
  1833	
  1834	    const loopedIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY(scrollY);
  1835	
  1836	    return ((loopedIndex % options.length) + options.length) % options.length;
  1837	  };
  1838	
  1839	  const setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex = (fieldKey, loopedIndex) => {
  1840	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleIndexes((currentIndexes) => {
  1841	      if (currentIndexes[fieldKey] === loopedIndex) {
  1842	        return currentIndexes;
  1843	      }
  1844	
  1845	      return {
  1846	        ...currentIndexes,
  1847	        [fieldKey]: loopedIndex,
  1848	      };
  1849	    });
  1850	  };
  1851	
  1852	  const triggerDeliveryTimeWheelTick = (fieldKey, loopedIndex) => {
  1853	    if (
  1854	      Platform.OS !== "android" ||
  1855	      !deliveryTimeWheelIsDraggingRef.current[fieldKey] ||
  1856	      deliveryTimeWheelHapticIndexesRef.current[fieldKey] === loopedIndex
  1857	    ) {
  1858	      return;
  1859	    }
  1860	
  1861	    deliveryTimeWheelHapticIndexesRef.current[fieldKey] = loopedIndex;
  1862	    triggerShopInteractionTick();
  1863	  };
  1864	
  1865	  const updateDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleIndex = (fieldKey, scrollY) => {
  1866	    const loopedIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndexFromScrollY(scrollY);
  1867	
  1868	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex(fieldKey, loopedIndex);
  1869	    triggerDeliveryTimeWheelTick(fieldKey, loopedIndex);
  1870	  };
  1871	
  1872	  const scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex = (
  1873	    fieldKey,
  1874	    optionIndex,
  1875	    options,
  1876	    animated = true,
  1877	  ) => {
  1878	    const offset =
  1879	      getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex(options, optionIndex) *
  1880	      deliveryTimeWheelScrollStepHeight;
  1881	
  1882	    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  1883	      const scrollNode = deliveryTimeWheelScrollRefs.current[fieldKey];
  1884	
  1885	      if (scrollNode?.scrollToOffset) {
  1886	        scrollNode.scrollToOffset({ animated, offset });
  1887	        return;
  1888	      }
  1889	
  1890	      scrollNode?.scrollTo?.({
  1891	        animated,
  1892	        y: offset,
  1893	      });
  1894	    });
  1895	  };
  1896	
  1897	  const setDeliveryTimeWheelValue = (
  1898	    fieldKey,
  1899	    option,
  1900	    options,
  1901	    shouldScroll = true,
  1902	  ) => {
  1903	    setDeliveryFieldValues((currentValues) => ({
  1904	      ...currentValues,
  1905	      [fieldKey]: option,
  1906	    }));
  1907	    setActiveDeliveryFieldKey(fieldKey);
  1908	    setIsDeliveryCityDropdownOpen(false);
  1909	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  1910	    setOpenDeliveryTimeDropdownKey(null);
  1911	    setIsPaymentIssuerDropdownOpen(false);
  1912	    setDeliveryTimeWheelVisibleLoopedIndex(
  1913	      fieldKey,
  1914	      getDeliveryTimeWheelLoopedIndex(
  1915	        options,
  1916	        Math.max(0, options.indexOf(option)),
  1917	      ),
  1918	    );
  1919	
  1920	    if (shouldScroll) {
  1921	      scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex(
  1922	        fieldKey,
  1923	        Math.max(0, options.indexOf(option)),
  1924	        options,
  1925	        true,
  1926	      );
  1927	    }
  1928	  };
  1929	
  1930	  const settleDeliveryTimeWheel = (fieldKey, options, scrollY) => {
  1931	    const optionIndex = getDeliveryTimeWheelOptionIndexFromScrollY(
  1932	      options,
  1933	      scrollY,
  1934	    );
  1935	
  1936	    setDeliveryTimeWheelValue(fieldKey, options[optionIndex], options, false);
  1937	    deliveryTimeWheelIsDraggingRef.current[fieldKey] = false;
  1938	    scrollDeliveryTimeWheelToIndex(fieldKey, optionIndex, options, false);
  1939	  };

  2044	        );

  2047

  2052	        );
  2053	        return;
  2054	      }
  2055

  2057	        showPaymentAlert(
  2058	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  2059	          "This iPhone is not ready for Apple Pay. Make sure Wallet has an active card, then try again.",
  2060	        );
  2061	      }
  2062	
  2063	      return;
  2064	    }
  2065	
  2066	    if (Platform.OS !== "android") {
  2067	      showPaymentAlert(
  2068	        "Google Pay unavailable",
  2069	        "Google Pay is only available on Android. Use Debit/Credit Card on this device.",
  2070	      );
  2071	      return;
  2072	    }
  2073	
  2074	    if (isExpoGo) {
  2075	      showPaymentAlert(
  2076	        "Google Pay needs a development build",
  2077	        "Expo Go cannot open the native Google Pay sheet. Use an Android development build or Play Store build to test Google Pay.",
  2078	      );
  2079	      return;
  2080	    }
  2081	
  2082	    if (!isGooglePaySupported) {

  2086	      );

  2088	  };
  2089	
  2090	  const acceptPaymentCardDetailsOverlay = async () => {
  2091	    const latestCardDetails = stripeCardDetailsRef.current || stripeCardDetails;
  2092

  2094	      setAcceptedStripePaymentMethodId(null);

  2102

  2104

  2106	      paymentMethodResult = await createPaymentMethod({
  2107	        paymentMethodType: "Card",
  2108	        paymentMethodData: {
  2109	          billingDetails: buildStripeBillingDetails(),
  2110	        },
  2111	      });
  2112	    } catch (error) {
  2113	      setAcceptedStripePaymentMethodId(null);
  2114	      setIsPaymentCardAccepted(false);
  2115	      showPaymentAlert(
  2116	        "Card details needed",
  2117	        error?.localizedMessage ||
  2118	          error?.message ||
  2119	          "Stripe could not save those card details. Please check the card number, expiration date, and CVV.",
  2120	      );
  2121	      return;
  2122	    }
  2123	
  2124	    if (paymentMethodResult.error || !paymentMethodResult.paymentMethod?.id) {
  2125	      setAcceptedStripePaymentMethodId(null);
  2126	      setIsPaymentCardAccepted(false);
  2127	      showPaymentAlert(
  2128	        "Card details needed",
  2129	        paymentMethodResult.error?.localizedMessage ||
  2130	          paymentMethodResult.error?.message ||
  2131	          "Stripe could not save those card details. Please check the card number, expiration date, and CVV.",
  2132	      );
  2133	      return;
  2134	    }
  2135	
  2136	    setStripeCardDetails(latestCardDetails);
  2137	    setAcceptedStripePaymentMethodId(paymentMethodResult.paymentMethod.id);
  2138	    setSelectedPaymentOverlayMethod(paymentOverlayCardMethod);
  2139	    setIsPaymentCardAccepted(true);
  2140	    setIsPaymentCardDetailsOverlayVisible(false);
  2141	  };

  2324	  const startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger = (direction) => {
  2325	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2326	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
  2327	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2328	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2329	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
  2330	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(1);
  2331	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(
  2332	      direction === "left" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0,
  2333	    );
  2334	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(
  2335	      direction === "right" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0,
  2336	    );
  2337	  };
  2338	
  2339	  const showOverlayHeldArrows = () => {
  2340	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2341	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
  2342	  };
  2343	
  2344	  const hideOverlayHeldArrows = () => {
  2345	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
  2346	    overlayHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
  2347	  };
  2348	
  2349	  const overlayVisibleArrowOpacity = overlayHeldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
  2350	    inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
  2351	    outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
  2352	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2353	  });
  2354	  const overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
  2355	    overlayVisibleArrowOpacity,
  2356	    overlayDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.interpolate({
  2357	      inputRange: [0, 1],
  2358	      outputRange: [1, 0],
  2359	      extrapolate: "clamp",
  2360	    }),
  2361	  );
  2362	  const overlayLeftArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
  2363	    overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity,
  2364	    overlayDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity,
  2365	  ).interpolate({
  2366	    inputRange: [0, 1],
  2367	    outputRange: [0, 1],
  2368	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2369	  });
  2370	  const overlayRightArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
  2371	    overlayDirectionalBaseArrowOpacity,
  2372	    overlayDirectionalRightArrowOpacity,
  2373	  ).interpolate({
  2374	    inputRange: [0, 1],
  2375	    outputRange: [0, 1],
  2376	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2377	  });
  2378	  const overlayImageTravelDistance = Math.max(
  2379	    overlayImageStageWidth / 2 + piccolaOverlayImageHalfSize,
  2380	    150,
  2381	  );
  2382	  const overlayIncomingStartOffset =
  2383	    overlayImageDirection < 0
  2384	      ? overlayImageTravelDistance
  2385	      : -overlayImageTravelDistance;
  2386	  const overlayOutgoingEndOffset = -overlayIncomingStartOffset;
  2387	  const overlayIncomingImageTranslateX = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2388	    inputRange: [0, 1],
  2389	    outputRange: [overlayIncomingStartOffset, 0],
  2390	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2391	  });
  2392	  const overlayOutgoingImageTranslateX = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2393	    inputRange: [0, 1],
  2394	    outputRange: [0, overlayOutgoingEndOffset],
  2395	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2396	  });
  2397	  const overlayIncomingImageOpacity = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2398	    inputRange: [0, 0.52, 0.82, 1],
  2399	    outputRange: [0, 0.08, 0.68, 1],
  2400	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2401	  });
  2402	  const overlayOutgoingImageOpacity = overlayImageProgress.interpolate({
  2403	    inputRange: [0, 0.18, 0.36, 1],
  2404	    outputRange: [1, 0.22, 0, 0],
  2405	    extrapolate: "clamp",
  2406	  });
  2407	
  2408	  const getOverlayProductTransitionDirection = (fromName, toName) => {
  2409	    const fromIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2410	      (product) => product.name === fromName,
  2411	    );
  2412	    const toIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2413	      (product) => product.name === toName,
  2414	    );
  2415	
  2416	    if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) {
  2417	      return -1;
  2418	    }
  2419	
  2420	    const lastOverlayIndex = overlayNavProducts.length - 1;
  2421	
  2422	    if (fromIndex === lastOverlayIndex && toIndex === 0) return -1;
  2423	    if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastOverlayIndex) return 1;
  2424	
  2425	    return toIndex > fromIndex ? -1 : 1;
  2426	  };
  2427	
  2428	  const transitionOverlayProductImage = (nextProductName, direction) => {
  2429	    if (!nextProductName || nextProductName === activeOverlayProductName) {
  2430	      return;
  2431	    }
  2432	
  2433	    if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current) {
  2434	      const previousAnimation = overlayImageAnimationRef.current;
  2435	      overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
  2436	      previousAnimation.stop();
  2437	    }
  2438	
  2439	    if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
  2440	      const previousNavAnimation = overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current;
  2441	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  2442	      previousNavAnimation.stop();
  2443	    }
  2444	
  2445	    const nextOverlayNavIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2446	      (product) => product.name === nextProductName,
  2447	    );
  2448	
  2449	    discardUnconfirmedOverlayProductDraft(activeOverlayProductName);
  2450	    setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(activeOverlayProductName);
  2451	    setOverlayImageDirection(direction);
  2452	    overlayImageProgress.setValue(0);
  2453	    setActiveOverlayProductName(nextProductName);
  2454	
  2455	    if (nextOverlayNavIndex >= 0) {
  2456	      const navAnimation = Animated.timing(overlayNavIndicatorProgress, {
  2457	        toValue: nextOverlayNavIndex,
  2458	        duration: 320,
  2459	        easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
  2460	        useNativeDriver: true,
  2461	      });
  2462	
  2463	      overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = navAnimation;
  2464	      navAnimation.start(() => {
  2465	        if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current === navAnimation) {
  2466	          overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
  2467	        }
  2468	      });
  2469	    }
  2470	
  2471	    const animation = Animated.timing(overlayImageProgress, {
  2472	      toValue: 1,
  2473	      duration: 320,
  2474	      easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
  2475	      useNativeDriver: true,
  2476	    });
  2477	
  2478	    overlayImageAnimationRef.current = animation;
  2479	
  2480	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
  2481	      if (finished) {
  2482	        setOverlayImageOutgoingProductName(null);
  2483	      }
  2484	
  2485	      if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current === animation) {
  2486	        overlayImageAnimationRef.current = null;
  2487	      }
  2488	    });
  2489	  };
  2490	
  2491	  const handleOverlayProductNameSelect = (nextProductName) => {
  2492	    const direction = getOverlayProductTransitionDirection(
  2493	      activeOverlayProductName,
  2494	      nextProductName,
  2495	    );
  2496	
  2497	    transitionOverlayProductImage(nextProductName, direction);
  2498	  };

  2500	  const renderOverlayArrowChevron = (direction, muted = false) => (

  2512	  const goToOverlayPreviousProduct = () => {
  2513	    const currentIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2514	      (product) => product.name === activeOverlayProductName,
  2515	    );
  2516	    if (currentIndex < 0) return;
  2517	
  2518	    const previousIndex =
  2519	      (currentIndex + overlayNavProducts.length - 1) %
  2520	      overlayNavProducts.length;
  2521	    startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
  2522	    transitionOverlayProductImage(overlayNavProducts[previousIndex].name, 1);
  2523	  };
  2524	
  2525	  const goToOverlayNextProduct = () => {
  2526	    const currentIndex = overlayNavProducts.findIndex(
  2527	      (product) => product.name === activeOverlayProductName,
  2528	    );
  2529	    if (currentIndex < 0) return;
  2530	
  2531	    const nextIndex = (currentIndex + 1) % overlayNavProducts.length;
  2532	    startOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
  2533	    transitionOverlayProductImage(overlayNavProducts[nextIndex].name, -1);
  2534	  };
  2535	
  2536	  const getOverlayBandTouchPoint = (event) => {
  2537	    const primaryTouch =
  2538	      event?.nativeEvent?.touches?.[0] ||
  2539	      event?.nativeEvent?.changedTouches?.[0] ||
  2540	      event?.nativeEvent;
  2541	
  2542	    return {
  2543	      x:
  2544	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageX === "number"
  2545	          ? primaryTouch.pageX
  2546	          : primaryTouch?.locationX,
  2547	      y:
  2548	        typeof primaryTouch?.pageY === "number"
  2549	          ? primaryTouch.pageY
  2550	          : primaryTouch?.locationY,
  2551	    };
  2552	  };
  2553	
  2554	  const handleOverlayBandTouchStart = (event) => {
  2555	    const { x, y } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
  2556	
  2557	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = x;
  2558	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = y;
  2559	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = false;
  2560	    showOverlayHeldArrows();
  2561	  };
  2562	
  2563	  const handleOverlayBandTouchMove = (event) => {
  2564	    if (overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current) return;
  2565	
  2566	    const startX = overlaySwipeStartXRef.current;
  2567	    const startY = overlaySwipeStartYRef.current;
  2568	    const { x: currentX, y: currentY } = getOverlayBandTouchPoint(event);
  2569	
  2570	    if (
  2571	      typeof startX !== "number" ||
  2572	      typeof startY !== "number" ||
  2573	      typeof currentX !== "number" ||
  2574	      typeof currentY !== "number"
  2575	    ) {
  2576	      return;
  2577	    }
  2578	
  2579	    const dx = currentX - startX;
  2580	    const dy = Math.abs(currentY - startY);
  2581	    const minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance = 12.5;
  2582	    const horizontalDominanceRatio = 0.4375;
  2583	    const isHorizontalSwipeIntent =
  2584	      Math.abs(dx) >= minimumHorizontalSwipeDistance &&
  2585	      Math.abs(dx) > dy * horizontalDominanceRatio;
  2586	
  2587	    if (!isHorizontalSwipeIntent) return;
  2588	
  2589	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = true;
  2590	
  2591	    if (dx < 0) {
  2592	      goToOverlayNextProduct();
  2593	    } else {
  2594	      goToOverlayPreviousProduct();
  2595	    }
  2596	  };
  2597	
  2598	  const handleOverlayBandTouchEnd = () => {
  2599	    overlaySwipeStartXRef.current = null;
  2600	    overlaySwipeStartYRef.current = null;
  2601	    overlaySwipeCommittedRef.current = false;
  2602	    hideOverlayHeldArrows();
  2603	  };
  2604	
  2605	  useEffect(() => {

  2610	    overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
  2611	      clearOverlayDirectionalArrowLinger();
  2612	      overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current = null;
  2613	    }, 130);
  2614	
  2615	    return () => {
  2616	      if (overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current) {
  2617	        clearTimeout(overlayDirectionalArrowResetTimeoutRef.current);
  2618	      }
  2619	    };
  2620	  }, [activeOverlayProductName]);
  2621	
  2622	  useEffect(() => {
  2623	    return () => {
  2624	      if (overlayImageAnimationRef.current) {
  2625	        overlayImageAnimationRef.current.stop();
  2626	      }
  2627	
  2628	      if (overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current) {
  2629	        overlayNavIndicatorAnimationRef.current.stop();
  2630	      }
  2631	    };
  2632	  }, []);

  3709	      shippingPreviewActionBandAnimationRef.current = null;

  3714	      deliveryOverlayShakeAnimationRef.current = null;

  3717	    products.forEach((product) => {

  3730	    setIsCartOverlayVisible(false);

  3732	    setIsTimeOverlayVisible(false);
  3733	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);

  3749	    setSelectedDeliveryState("");

  3756	    setDeliveryFieldValues(defaultDeliveryFieldValues);
  3757	    setEmptyTouchedDeliveryFieldKeys({});

  3794	  const showPaymentOrderConfirmationPrompt = () => {

  3804	  const showOrderPlacementConfirmation = () => {

  3807	    setIsContactOverlayVisible(false);
  3808	    setIsTimeOverlayVisible(false);
  3809	    setIsDeliveryOverlayVisible(false);
  3810	    setIsPaymentOverlayVisible(false);
  3811	    setIsPaymentCardDetailsOverlayVisible(false);
  3812	    setIsPaymentPayPalOverlayVisible(false);
  3813	    setIsPaymentOrderConfirmationVisible(false);
  3814	    setIsPlaceholderOverlayVisible(true);
  3815	    setIsOrderPlacementConfirmed(true);
  3816	    setIsDeliveryStateDropdownOpen(false);
  3817	  };
  3818	  const handleOrderConfirmationYesPress = async () => {
  3819	    if (isStripePaymentInFlight) return;
  3820	
  3821	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
  3822	      showPaymentAlert(
  3823	        "Payment unavailable",
  3824	        "Stripe payments are available in the mobile app build.",
  3825	      );
  3826	      return;
  3827	    }
  3828	
  3829	    const configurationIssue = getStripeConfigurationIssue();
  3830	
  3831	    if (configurationIssue) {
  3832	      showPaymentAlert("Stripe setup needed", configurationIssue);
  3833	      return;
  3834	    }
  3835	
  3836	    if (
  3837	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayCardMethod &&
  3838	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod &&
  3839	      selectedPaymentOverlayMethod !== paymentOverlayApplePayMethod
  3840	    ) {
  3841	      showPaymentAlert(
  3842	        "Payment method unavailable",
  3843	        "Use Debit/Credit Card, Google Pay, or Apple Pay for this Stripe checkout.",
  3844	      );
  3845	      return;
  3846	    }
  3847	
  3848	    if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayApplePayMethod) {
  3849	      if (Platform.OS !== "ios") {
  3850	        showPaymentAlert(
  3851	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  3852	          "Apple Pay is only available on iPhone. Use Debit/Credit Card or Google Pay on this device.",
  3853	        );
  3854	        return;
  3855	      }
  3856	
  3857	      if (isExpoGo) {
  3858	        showPaymentAlert(
  3859	          "Apple Pay needs a development build",
  3860	          "Expo Go cannot open the native Apple Pay sheet. Use an iOS development build or App Store build to test Apple Pay.",
  3861	        );
  3862	        return;
  3863	      }
  3864	
  3865	      if (!stripeMerchantIdentifier) {
  3866	        showPaymentAlert(
  3867	          "Apple Pay setup needed",
  3868	          "Add EXPO_PUBLIC_STRIPE_MERCHANT_IDENTIFIER to mob/.env so StripeProvider can use your Apple merchant ID.",
  3869	        );
  3870	        return;
  3871	      }
  3872	
  3873	      if (!isApplePaySupported) {
  3874	        showPaymentAlert(
  3875	          "Apple Pay unavailable",
  3876	          "This iPhone is not ready for Apple Pay. Make sure Wallet has an active card, then try again.",
  3877	        );
  3878	        return;
  3879	      }
  3880	    } else if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod) {
  3881	      if (Platform.OS !== "android") {
  3882	        showPaymentAlert(
  3883	          "Google Pay unavailable",
  3884	          "Google Pay is only available on Android. Use Debit/Credit Card on this device.",
  3885	        );
  3886	        return;
  3887	      }
  3888	
  3889	      if (isExpoGo) {
  3890	        showPaymentAlert(
  3891	          "Google Pay needs a development build",
  3892	          "Expo Go cannot open the native Google Pay sheet. Use an Android development build or Play Store build to test Google Pay.",
  3893	        );
  3894	        return;
  3895	      }
  3896	
  3897	      if (!isGooglePaySupported) {
  3898	        showPaymentAlert(
  3899	          "Google Pay unavailable",
  3900	          "This device is not ready for Google Pay. Make sure Google Wallet is set up, then try again.",
  3901	        );
  3902	        return;
  3903	      }
  3904	    } else if (
  3905	      !isPaymentCardAccepted ||
  3906	      !stripeCardDetails?.complete ||
  3907	      !acceptedStripePaymentMethodId
  3908	    ) {
  3909	      showPaymentAlert(
  3910	        "Card details needed",
  3911	        "Enter a complete card number, expiration date, and CVV.",
  3912	      );
  3913	      return;
  3914	    }
  3915	
  3916	    const orderPayload = buildStripeOrderPayload();
  3917	
  3918	    if (orderPayload.items.length === 0) {
  3919	      showPaymentAlert("Cart is empty", "Add an item before placing an order.");
  3920	      return;
  3921	    }
  3922	
  3923	    setIsStripePaymentInFlight(true);
  3924	
  3925	    try {
  3926	      const paymentSheet = await createStripePaymentSheet(orderPayload);
  3927	      if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayApplePayMethod) {
  3928	        await confirmApplePayPayment(
  3929	          paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret,
  3930	          paymentSheet,
  3931	        );
  3932	      } else if (selectedPaymentOverlayMethod === paymentOverlayGooglePayMethod) {
  3933	        await confirmGooglePayPayment(paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret);
  3934	      } else {
  3935	        const confirmResult = await confirmPayment(
  3936	          paymentSheet.paymentIntentClientSecret,
  3937	          {
  3938	            paymentMethodType: "Card",
  3939	            paymentMethodData: {
  3940	              billingDetails: buildStripeBillingDetails(),
  3941	              paymentMethodId: acceptedStripePaymentMethodId,
  3942	            },
  3943	          },
  3944	        );

  3947	          throw new Error(
  3948	            confirmResult.error.localizedMessage ||
  3949	              confirmResult.error.message ||
  3950	              "Payment was not completed.",
  3951	          );
  3952	        }
  3953	
  3954	        if (
  3955	          confirmResult.paymentIntent?.status &&
  3956	          !["Succeeded", "Processing"].includes(
  3957	            confirmResult.paymentIntent.status,
  3958	          )
  3959	        ) {
  3960	          throw new Error(
  3961	            `Payment status is ${confirmResult.paymentIntent.status}. Please try again.`,
  3962	          );
  3963	        }
  3964	      }
  3965	
  3966	      showOrderPlacementConfirmation();
  3967	    } catch (error) {
  3968	      showPaymentAlert(
  3969	        "Payment not completed",
  3970	        error?.message || "Please try again.",
  3971	      );
  3972	    } finally {
  3973	      setIsStripePaymentInFlight(false);
  3974	    }

    96	function getActivePageFromPath(pathname) {
    97	  if (pathname === "/products") return "products";
