   104	function getNavigationDirection(fromPage, toPage) {
   105	  const fromIndex = navPages.indexOf(fromPage);
   106	  const toIndex = navPages.indexOf(toPage);
   107	
   108	  if (fromIndex < 0 || toIndex < 0 || fromIndex === toIndex) return 1;
   109	
   110	  const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   111	
   112	  if (fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0) return 1;
   113	  if (fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex) return -1;
   114	
   115	  return toIndex > fromIndex ? 1 : -1;
   116	}
   117	
   118	export default function AppHeader({
   119	  activePage,
   120	  dimHeaderExceptShopButton = false,
   121	  scrollY,
   122	  showCarousel = true,
   123	  showHero = true,
   124	  showOnlyCarousel = false,
   125	  showOnlyHero = false,
   126	}) {
   127	  const pathname = usePathname();
   128	  const safeAreaInsets = useSafeAreaInsets();
   129	  const backgroundHeroState = useBackgroundHeroState();
   130	  const screenSwipe = useHeaderSwipe();
   131	  const resolvedActivePage = activePage || getActivePageFromPath(pathname);
   132	  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();
   133	  const activePageIndex = Math.max(navPages.indexOf(resolvedActivePage), 0);
   134	  const handlesCarouselVisuals = showCarousel && !showOnlyHero;
   135	  const androidHeaderTopOverlap =
   136	    getSmallAndroidHeaderTopOverlap(safeAreaInsets);
   137	  const androidHeaderTopOverlapStyle = androidHeaderTopOverlap
   138	    ? { marginTop: -androidHeaderTopOverlap }
   139	    : null;
   140	
   141	  const fallbackScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   142	  const safeScrollY = scrollY || fallbackScrollY;
   143	  const canNavigateWithHeader = useHeaderNavigationGate(safeScrollY);
   144	  const heroStateScrollY =
   145	    showHero && backgroundHeroState?.isFrozen && backgroundHeroState?.heroScrollY
   146	      ? backgroundHeroState.heroScrollY
   147	      : safeScrollY;
   148	  const headerMotionScrollY = showOnlyHero ? heroStateScrollY : safeScrollY;
   149	  const frozenHeroMotionScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   150	  const frozenHeaderMotionScrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   151	  const shouldFreezeHeaderMotion = Boolean(
   152	    screenSwipe?.isActive || screenSwipe?.routeTransitionState?.isActive
   153	  );
   154	  const previousShouldFreezeHeaderMotionRef = useRef(false);
   155	
   156	  useEffect(() => {
   157	    if (
   158	      shouldFreezeHeaderMotion &&
   159	      !previousShouldFreezeHeaderMotionRef.current
   160	    ) {
   161	      frozenHeroMotionScrollY.setValue(readAnimatedValue(heroStateScrollY));
   162	      frozenHeaderMotionScrollY.setValue(readAnimatedValue(headerMotionScrollY));
   163	    }
   164	
   165	    previousShouldFreezeHeaderMotionRef.current = shouldFreezeHeaderMotion;
   166	  }, [
   167	    frozenHeroMotionScrollY,
   168	    frozenHeaderMotionScrollY,
   169	    headerMotionScrollY,
   170	    heroStateScrollY,
   171	    shouldFreezeHeaderMotion,
   172	  ]);
   173	
   174	  const resolvedHeroMotionScrollY = shouldFreezeHeaderMotion
   175	    ? frozenHeroMotionScrollY
   176	    : heroStateScrollY;
   177	  const resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY = shouldFreezeHeaderMotion
   178	    ? frozenHeaderMotionScrollY
   179	    : headerMotionScrollY;
   180	
   181	  const fallbackHeldArrowOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
   182	  const fallbackRouteTransitionProgress = useRef(
   183	    new Animated.Value(0)
   184	  ).current;
   185	  const heldArrowOpacity =
   186	    screenSwipe?.heldArrowOpacity || fallbackHeldArrowOpacity;
   187	  const routeTransitionProgress =
   188	    screenSwipe?.routeTransitionProgress || fallbackRouteTransitionProgress;
   189	  const indicatorProgress = useRef(new Animated.Value(activePageIndex)).current;
   190	  const fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity = useRef(
   191	    new Animated.Value(0)
   192	  ).current;
   193	  const fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity = useRef(
   194	    new Animated.Value(0)
   195	  ).current;
   196	  const fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression = useRef(
   197	    new Animated.Value(0)
   198	  ).current;
   199	  const directionalLeftArrowOpacity =
   200	    screenSwipe?.directionalLeftArrowOpacity ||
   201	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity;
   202	  const directionalRightArrowOpacity =
   203	    screenSwipe?.directionalRightArrowOpacity ||
   204	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity;
   205	  const directionalArrowBaseSuppression =
   206	    screenSwipe?.directionalArrowBaseSuppression ||
   207	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression;
   208	
   209	  const indicatorAnimationRef = useRef(null);
   210	  const indicatorIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   211	  const suppressCarouselPressUntilRef = useRef(0);
   212	  const canNavigateWithHeaderRef = useRef(canNavigateWithHeader);
   213	
   214	  const activePageRef = useRef(resolvedActivePage);
   215	  const activeIndexRef = useRef(activePageIndex);
   216	  canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current = canNavigateWithHeader;
   217	  activePageRef.current = resolvedActivePage;
   218	  activeIndexRef.current = activePageIndex;
   219	
   220	  useEffect(() => {
   221	    if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return;
   222	
   223	    const resolvedCheeseboardImage = Image.resolveAssetSource(
   224	      cheeseboardProductsImage
   225	    );
   226	
   227	    if (resolvedCheeseboardImage?.uri) {
   228	      Image.prefetch(resolvedCheeseboardImage.uri).catch(() => {});
   229	    }
   230	  }, []);
   231	
   232	  const showHeldArrows = (event = null) => {
   233	    if (screenSwipe?.showHeldArrowHint) {
   234	      screenSwipe.showHeldArrowHint(event);
   235	      return;
   236	    }
   237	
   238	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   239	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(arrowHintPeakOpacity);
   240	  };
   241	
   242	  const hideHeldArrows = () => {
   243	    if (screenSwipe?.hideHeldArrowHint) {
   244	      screenSwipe.hideHeldArrowHint();
   245	      return;
   246	    }
   247	
   248	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   249	    fallbackHeldArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   250	  };
   251	
   252	  const cancelHeldArrows = () => {
   253	    if (screenSwipe?.cancelHeldArrowHint) {
   254	      screenSwipe.cancelHeldArrowHint();
   255	      return;
   256	    }
   257	
   258	    hideHeldArrows();
   259	  };
   260	
   261	  const updateHeldArrowsFromTouchMove = (event) => {
   262	    screenSwipe?.updateHeldArrowHintMovement?.(event);
   263	    return false;
   264	  };
   265	
   266	  const clearDirectionalArrowLinger = () => {
   267	    if (screenSwipe?.clearDirectionalArrowLinger) {
   268	      screenSwipe.clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   269	      return;
   270	    }
   271	
   272	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   273	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   274	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   275	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   276	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(0);
   277	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(0);
   278	  };
   279	
   280	  const startDirectionalArrowLinger = (direction) => {
   281	    if (screenSwipe?.startDirectionalArrowLinger) {
   282	      screenSwipe.startDirectionalArrowLinger(direction);
   283	      return;
   284	    }
   285	
   286	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   287	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.stopAnimation();
   288	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.stopAnimation();
   289	    fallbackDirectionalArrowBaseSuppression.setValue(1);
   290	    fallbackDirectionalLeftArrowOpacity.setValue(
   291	      direction === "left" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   292	    );
   293	    fallbackDirectionalRightArrowOpacity.setValue(
   294	      direction === "right" ? arrowHintPeakOpacity : 0
   295	    );
   296	  };
   297	
   298	  const updateSwipePreview = (dragDistance) => {
   299	    if (!screenSwipe) return;
   300	
   301	    screenSwipe.updateSwipe({
   302	      x: dragDistance,
   303	    });
   304	  };
   305	
   306	  const animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes = (fromIndex, toIndex) => {
   307	    if (fromIndex === toIndex) {
   308	      if (!indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   309	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   310	      }
   311	      return;
   312	    }
   313	
   314	    if (indicatorAnimationRef.current) {
   315	      const previousAnimation = indicatorAnimationRef.current;
   316	      indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   317	      previousAnimation.stop();
   318	    }
   319	
   320	    indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   321	
   322	    const createIndicatorTiming = (toValue, duration = indicatorSlideDuration) =>
   323	      Animated.timing(indicatorProgress, {
   324	        toValue,
   325	        duration,
   326	        useNativeDriver: true,
   327	      });
   328	
   329	    const lastPageIndex = navPages.length - 1;
   330	    const animation =
   331	      fromIndex === lastPageIndex && toIndex === 0
   332	        ? Animated.sequence([
   333	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length),
   334	            createIndicatorTiming(-1, 0),
   335	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   336	          ])
   337	        : fromIndex === 0 && toIndex === lastPageIndex
   338	        ? Animated.sequence([
   339	            createIndicatorTiming(-1),
   340	            createIndicatorTiming(navPages.length, 0),
   341	            createIndicatorTiming(toIndex),
   342	          ])
   343	        : createIndicatorTiming(toIndex);
   344	
   345	    indicatorAnimationRef.current = animation;
   346	
   347	    animation.start(({ finished }) => {
   348	      if (finished) {
   349	        indicatorIndexRef.current = toIndex;
   350	      }
   351	
   352	      if (indicatorAnimationRef.current === animation) {
   353	        indicatorAnimationRef.current = null;
   354	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   355	      }
   356	    });
   357	  };
   358	
   359	  useEffect(() => {
   360	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(indicatorIndexRef.current, activePageIndex);
   361	  }, [activePageIndex, indicatorProgress]);
   362	
   363	  const animateIndicatorToPage = (pageName) => {
   364	    const pageIndex = navPages.indexOf(pageName);
   365	    if (pageIndex < 0) return;
   366	
   367	    animateIndicatorBetweenIndexes(activeIndexRef.current, pageIndex);
   368	  };
   369	
   370	  const goToPage = (pageName, animateIndicator = true) => {
   371	    hideHeldArrows();
   372	
   373	    if (pageName === activePageRef.current) {
   374	      if (pageName === "shop") {
   375	        goToPage("home", animateIndicator);
   376	      }
   377	
   378	      return;
   379	    }
   380	
   381	    const route = pageRoutes[pageName];
   382	    if (!route) return;
   383	
   384	    if (navPages.includes(pageName)) {
   385	      if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   386	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   387	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   388	        return;
   389	      }
   390	
   391	      backgroundHeroState?.freezeHero(safeScrollY);
   392	      if (animateIndicator) animateIndicatorToPage(pageName);
   393	      router.replace(route);
   394	      return;
   395	    }
   396	
   397	    router.push(route);
   398	  };
   399	
   400	  const goToPreviousPage = () => {
   401	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   402	
   403	    const previousIndex =
   404	      (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   405	
   406	    goToPage(navPages[previousIndex], true, -1);
   407	  };
   408	
   409	  const goToNextPage = () => {
   410	    if (Date.now() < suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current) return;
   411	
   412	    const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   413	
   414	    goToPage(navPages[nextIndex], true, 1);
   415	  };
   416	
   417	  const shouldClaimCarouselSwipe = (_, gestureState) => {
   418	    if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return false;
   419	
   420	    const horizontalDistance = Math.abs(gestureState.dx);
   421	    const verticalDistance = Math.abs(gestureState.dy);
   422	
   423	    if (
   424	      horizontalDistance >= tapHoldHorizontalCancelDistance &&
   425	      horizontalDistance > verticalDistance * tapHoldHorizontalDominanceRatio
   426	    ) {
   427	      cancelHeldArrows();
   428	    }
   429	
   430	    const shouldClaim =
   431	      horizontalDistance > carouselSwipeActivationDistance &&
   432	      horizontalDistance > verticalDistance * carouselSwipeActivationRatio;
   433	
   434	    if (shouldClaim) {
   435	      cancelHeldArrows();
   436	    }
   437	
   438	    return shouldClaim;
   439	  };
   440	
   441	  const carouselPanResponder = useRef(
   442	    PanResponder.create({
   443	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: shouldClaimCarouselSwipe,
   444	
   445	      onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: shouldClaimCarouselSwipe,
   446	
   447	      onPanResponderTerminationRequest: (_, gestureState) =>
   448	        !shouldClaimCarouselSwipe(null, gestureState),
   449	
   450	      onPanResponderMove: (_, gestureState) => {
   451	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) return;
   452	
   453	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   454	        updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   455	
   456	        if (gestureState.dx <= -carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   457	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   458	          return;
   459	        }
   460	
   461	        if (gestureState.dx >= carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   462	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   463	          return;
   464	        }
   465	
   466	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   467	      },
   468	
   469	      onPanResponderRelease: (_, gestureState) => {
   470	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   471	        hideHeldArrows();
   472	
   473	        if (!canNavigateWithHeaderRef.current?.()) {
   474	          clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   475	          screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   476	          return;
   477	        }
   478	
   479	        if (gestureState.dx <= -carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   480	          const nextIndex = (activeIndexRef.current + 1) % navPages.length;
   481	          const nextPage = navPages[nextIndex];
   482	
   483	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   484	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   485	            x: gestureState.dx,
   486	            page: nextPage,
   487	            direction: 1,
   488	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   489	          });
   490	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("left");
   491	          goToPage(nextPage, true, 1);
   492	
   493	          return;
   494	        }
   495	
   496	        if (gestureState.dx >= carouselSwipeCommitDistance) {
   497	          const previousIndex =
   498	            (activeIndexRef.current + navPages.length - 1) % navPages.length;
   499	          const previousPage = navPages[previousIndex];
   500	
   501	          updateSwipePreview(gestureState.dx);
   502	          screenSwipe?.commitSwipe({
   503	            x: gestureState.dx,
   504	            page: previousPage,
   505	            direction: -1,
   506	            fromPage: activePageRef.current,
   507	          });
   508	          startDirectionalArrowLinger("right");
   509	          goToPage(previousPage, true, -1);
   510	
   511	          return;
   512	        }
   513	
   514	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   515	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   516	      },
   517	
   518	      onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
   519	        suppressCarouselPressUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 500;
   520	        hideHeldArrows();
   521	        clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   522	        screenSwipe?.clearSwipe({ animate: true });
   523	      },
   524	
   525	      onShouldBlockNativeResponder: () => true,
   526	    })
   527	  ).current;
   528	
   529	  useEffect(() => {
   530	    hideHeldArrows();
   531	
   532	    return () => {
   533	      hideHeldArrows();
   534	    };
   535	  }, [resolvedActivePage, showCarousel, showOnlyHero]);
   536	
   537	  useEffect(
   538	    () => () => {
   539	      clearDirectionalArrowLinger();
   540	    },
   541	    []
   542	  );
   543	
   544	  const activeLink = pageLabels[resolvedActivePage] || "Home";
   545	  const arrowLinkTranslateX = 0;
   546	
   547	  const scrollBeganArrowVisibility = resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   548	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   549	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   550	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   551	  });
   552	  const oldHeaderArrowVisibility = resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   553	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   554	    outputRange: [1, 0],
   555	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   556	  });
   557	  const oldHeaderHeldArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   558	    heldArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   559	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   560	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   561	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   562	    }),
   563	    oldHeaderArrowVisibility
   564	  );
   565	  const scrolledStateArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   566	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility,
   567	    arrowHintPeakOpacity
   568	  );
   569	  const scrolledVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   570	    heldArrowOpacity,
   571	    scrolledStateArrowOpacity
   572	  );
   573	  const gatedScrolledArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   574	    scrolledVisibleArrowOpacity.interpolate({
   575	      inputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   576	      outputRange: [0, arrowHintPeakOpacity],
   577	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   578	    }),
   579	    scrollBeganArrowVisibility
   580	  );
   581	  const gatedVisibleArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   582	    oldHeaderHeldArrowOpacity,
   583	    gatedScrolledArrowOpacity
   584	  ).interpolate({
   585	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   586	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   587	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   588	  });
   589	  const directionalBaseArrowOpacity = Animated.multiply(
   590	    gatedVisibleArrowOpacity,
   591	    directionalArrowBaseSuppression.interpolate({
   592	      inputRange: [0, 1],
   593	      outputRange: [1, 0],
   594	      extrapolate: "clamp",
   595	    })
   596	  );
   597	  const leftArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   598	    directionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   599	    directionalLeftArrowOpacity
   600	  ).interpolate({
   601	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   602	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   603	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   604	  });
   605	  const rightArrowOpacity = Animated.add(
   606	    directionalBaseArrowOpacity,
   607	    directionalRightArrowOpacity
   608	  ).interpolate({
   609	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   610	    outputRange: [0, 1],
   611	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   612	  });
   613	
   614	  const heroVerticalFadeTranslateY = resolvedHeroMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   615	    inputRange: [0, heroVerticalFadeScrollDistance],
   616	    outputRange: [heroVerticalFadeHeight, -heroVerticalFadeFeatherHeight],
   617	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   618	  });
   619	
   620	  const stickyOffset =
   621	    Platform.OS === "web"
   622	      ? 0
   623	      : resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   624	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   625	          outputRange: [0, carouselStickyOffsetY],
   626	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   627	        });
   628	  const carouselExpansionOffsetY =
   629	    Platform.OS === "ios"
   630	      ? resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   631	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   632	          outputRange: [0, carouselExpansionHeight],
   633	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   634	        })
   635	      : stickyOffset;
   636	  const carouselInnerTranslateY = carouselExpansionOffsetY;
   637	
   638	  const centerShadowOpacity =
   639	    Platform.OS === "web"
   640	      ? 1
   641	      : resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   642	          inputRange: [96, 120],
   643	          outputRange: [0, 1],
   644	          extrapolate: "clamp",
   645	        });
   646	
   647	  const stickyExpansionTranslateY = resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   648	    inputRange: [96, 120],
   649	    outputRange: [-stickyExpansionMaxHeight, 0],
   650	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   651	  });
   652	
   653	  const heroScale = resolvedHeroMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   654	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   655	    outputRange: [heroStartScale, heroScaleAtMinimumOpacity],
   656	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   657	  });
   658	
   659	  const heroTranslateY = resolvedHeroMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   660	    inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   661	    outputRange: [heroStartTranslateY, heroTranslateYAtMinimumOpacity],
   662	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   663	  });
   664	
   665	  const headerTranslateY = resolvedHeaderMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   666	    inputRange: [0, 120],
   667	    outputRange: [0, -120],
   668	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   669	  });
   670	
   671	  const indicatorSegmentWidth = windowWidth / navPages.length;
   672	  const indicatorTranslateX = Animated.multiply(
   673	    indicatorProgress,
   674	    indicatorSegmentWidth
   675	  );
   676	  const isMainHeroPage = navPages.includes(resolvedActivePage);
   677	  const heroImageSource = isMainHeroPage
   678	    ? noCheeseboardHeroImage
   679	    : defaultHeroImage;
   680	  const mainHeroVisualOpacity = isMainHeroPage
   681	    ? resolvedHeroMotionScrollY.interpolate({
   682	        inputRange: [0, heroFadeScrollDistance],
   683	        outputRange: [1, heroMinimumScrollOpacity],
   684	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   685	      })
   686	    : 1;
   687	  const heroWidthFitScale = windowWidth / heroSourceWidth;
   688	  const heroWidthFitImageHeight = heroSourceHeight * heroWidthFitScale;
   689	  const heroImageFrameStyle = isMainHeroPage
   690	    ? {
   691	        width: windowWidth,
   692	        height: heroWidthFitImageHeight,
   693	      }
   694	    : null;
   695	  const heroImageTransformStyle = isMainHeroPage
   696	    ? null
   697	    : {
   698	        transform: [{ scale: heroScale }, { translateY: heroTranslateY }],
   699	      };
   700	  const heroImageOpacityStyle = isMainHeroPage
   701	    ? { opacity: mainHeroVisualOpacity }
   702	    : null;
   703	  const heroCoverScale = Math.max(
   704	    windowWidth / heroSourceWidth,
   705	    heroVerticalFadeHeight / heroSourceHeight
   706	  );
   707	  const heroLayoutScale = isMainHeroPage ? heroWidthFitScale : heroCoverScale;
   708	  const heroRenderedWidth = heroSourceWidth * heroLayoutScale;
   709	  const heroRenderedHeight = heroSourceHeight * heroLayoutScale;
   710	  const heroRenderedLeft = (windowWidth - heroRenderedWidth) / 2;
   711	  const heroRenderedTop = isMainHeroPage
   712	    ? 0
   713	    : (heroVerticalFadeHeight - heroRenderedHeight) / 2;
   714	  const noCheeseboardVisibleBottomY = Math.min(
   715	    heroVerticalFadeHeight,
   716	    heroRenderedTop + noCheeseboardVisibleBottomSourceY * heroLayoutScale
   717	  );
   718	  const cheeseboardWidth = cheeseboardSourceWidth * heroLayoutScale;
   719	  const cheeseboardHeight = cheeseboardSourceHeight * heroLayoutScale;
   720	  const cheeseboardLeft =
   721	    heroRenderedLeft + cheeseboardSourceX * heroLayoutScale;
   722	  const cheeseboardTop = isMainHeroPage
   723	    ? noCheeseboardVisibleBottomY - cheeseboardHeight / 2
   724	    : heroRenderedTop + cheeseboardSourceY * heroLayoutScale;
   725	  const routeTransitionState = screenSwipe?.routeTransitionState;
   726	  const isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled =
   727	    isMainHeroPage && isHeaderNewState(resolvedHeroMotionScrollY);
   728	  const isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive =
   729	    isMainHeroPage &&
   730	    !isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled &&
   731	    Boolean(routeTransitionState?.isActive);
   732	  const isCheeseboardSwipeActive =
   733	    isMainHeroPage &&
   734	    !isCheeseboardNavigationMotionDisabled &&
   735	    Boolean(screenSwipe?.isActive);
   736	  const cheeseboardTransitionDirection =
   737	    routeTransitionState?.direction === -1 ? -1 : 1;
   738	  const cheeseboardTransitionStartX =
   739	    typeof routeTransitionState?.startX === "number"
   740	      ? routeTransitionState.startX
   741	      : 0;
   742	  const cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX =
   743	    Platform.OS === "ios" ? 0.01 : 0;
   744	  const cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX =
   745	    Math.abs(cheeseboardTransitionStartX) > 0.01
   746	      ? cheeseboardTransitionStartX
   747	      : cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX;
   748	  const cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity =
   749	    Platform.OS === "ios" ? 0.001 : 0;
   750	  const cheeseboardDragTranslateX = isCheeseboardSwipeActive
   751	    ? screenSwipe?.swipeX || 0
   752	    : 0;
   753	  const cheeseboardBaseTranslateX = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   754	    ? 0
   755	    : cheeseboardDragTranslateX;
   756	  const cheeseboardBaseOpacity = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   757	    ? routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   758	        inputRange: [0, 0.92, 1],
   759	        outputRange: [
   760	          cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity,
   761	          cheeseboardHiddenHandoffOpacity,
   762	          1,
   763	        ],
   764	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   765	      })
   766	    : 1;
   767	  const cheeseboardOutgoingTranslateX = isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
   768	    ? routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   769	        inputRange: [0, 1],
   770	        outputRange: [
   771	          cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX,
   772	          -cheeseboardTransitionDirection * windowWidth,
   773	        ],
   774	        extrapolate: "clamp",
   775	      })
   776	    : cheeseboardDragTranslateX;
   777	  const cheeseboardIncomingTranslateX = routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   778	    inputRange: [0, 1],
   779	    outputRange: [
   780	      cheeseboardTransitionStartTranslateX +
   781	        cheeseboardTransitionDirection * windowWidth,
   782	      cheeseboardCenteredMotionTranslateX,
   783	    ],
   784	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   785	  });
   786	  const cheeseboardIncomingOpacity = routeTransitionProgress.interpolate({
   787	    inputRange: [0, 0.92, 1],
   788	    outputRange: [1, 1, 0],
   789	    extrapolate: "clamp",
   790	  });
   791	  const cheeseboardPreviousPreviewTranslateX = screenSwipe?.swipeX
   792	    ? Animated.add(screenSwipe.swipeX, -windowWidth)
   793	    : -windowWidth;
   794	  const cheeseboardNextPreviewTranslateX = screenSwipe?.swipeX
   795	    ? Animated.add(screenSwipe.swipeX, windowWidth)
   796	    : windowWidth;
   797	  const shouldShowCheeseboardDragPreviews =
   798	    isMainHeroPage &&
   799	    isCheeseboardSwipeActive &&
   800	    !isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive;
   801	  const handleCarouselTouchStart = (event) => {
   802	    showHeldArrows(event);
   803	    return false;
   804	  };
   805	
   806	  const sharedHeaderTouchHandlers = {
   807	    onStartShouldSetResponderCapture: handleCarouselTouchStart,
   808	    onTouchStart: showHeldArrows,
   809	    onTouchMove: updateHeldArrowsFromTouchMove,
   810	    onTouchEnd: hideHeldArrows,
   811	    onTouchCancel: hideHeldArrows,
   812	  };
   813	
   814	  const renderArrowChevron = (direction) => (
   815	    <View
   816	      style={[
   817	        styles.arrowChevron,
   818	        direction === "left"
   819	          ? styles.arrowChevronLeft
   820	          : styles.arrowChevronRight,
   821	      ]}
   822	    />
   823	  );
   824	
   825	  const renderCheeseboardLayer = (key, translateX, opacity = 1) => {
   826	    const slideTransformStyle =
   827	      translateX === 0 ? null : { transform: [{ translateX }] };
   828	    const resolvedSlideOpacity =
   829	      isMainHeroPage && opacity !== 1
   830	        ? Animated.multiply(opacity, mainHeroVisualOpacity)
   831	        : isMainHeroPage
   832	        ? mainHeroVisualOpacity
   833	        : opacity;
   834	    const slideOpacityStyle =
   835	      resolvedSlideOpacity === 1 ? null : { opacity: resolvedSlideOpacity };
   836	
   837	    return (
   838	      <Animated.View
   839	        key={key}
   840	        collapsable={false}
   841	        pointerEvents="none"
   842	        renderToHardwareTextureAndroid
   843	        style={[
   844	          styles.cheeseboardSlideLayer,
   845	          slideTransformStyle,
   846	          slideOpacityStyle,
   847	        ]}
   848	      >
   849	        <Animated.View
   850	          collapsable={false}
   851	          style={styles.cheeseboardHeroTransformLayer}
   852	        >
   853	          <Animated.View
   854	            collapsable={false}
   855	            renderToHardwareTextureAndroid
   856	            style={[
   857	              styles.cheeseboardProductsFrame,
   858	              {
   859	                left: cheeseboardLeft,
   860	                top: cheeseboardTop,
   861	                width: cheeseboardWidth,
   862	                height: cheeseboardHeight,
   863	                transform: [{ scale: heroScale }],
   864	              },
   865	            ]}
   866	          >
   867	            <Image
   868	              fadeDuration={0}
   869	              source={cheeseboardProductsImage}
   870	              style={styles.cheeseboardProductsImage}
   871	              resizeMode="contain"
   872	            />
   873	          </Animated.View>
   874	        </Animated.View>
   875	      </Animated.View>
   876	    );
   877	  };
   878	
   879	  const carousel = (
   880	    <View
   881	      style={styles.carouselShell}
   882	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
   883	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
   884	    >
   885	      <Animated.View style={styles.carouselNavBar}>
   886	        <View style={styles.carouselStickyExpansion}>
   887	          <Animated.View
   888	            pointerEvents="none"
   889	            style={[
   890	              styles.carouselStickyExpansionFill,
   891	              {
   892	                transform: [{ translateY: stickyExpansionTranslateY }],
   893	              },
   894	            ]}
   895	          />
   896	        </View>
   897	
   898	        <Animated.View
   899	          style={[
   900	            styles.carouselInner,
   901	            {
   902	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselInnerTranslateY }],
   903	            },
   904	          ]}
   905	        >
   906	          <Pressable
   907	            onPress={goToPreviousPage}
   908	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   909	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   910	          >
   911	            <Animated.View
   912	              style={[
   913	                styles.arrowBox,
   914	                {
   915	                  opacity: leftArrowOpacity,
   916	                  transform: [
   917	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   918	                  ],
   919	                },
   920	              ]}
   921	            >
   922	              {renderArrowChevron("left")}
   923	            </Animated.View>
   924	          </Pressable>
   925	
   926	          <Pressable
   927	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   928	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   929	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   930	            style={styles.carouselActiveWrap}
   931	          >
   932	            <Text
   933	              numberOfLines={1}
   934	              adjustsFontSizeToFit
   935	              minimumFontScale={0.88}
   936	              style={[
   937	                styles.carouselActiveText,
   938	                styles.carouselActiveTextLayer,
   939	                {
   940	                  transform: [
   941	                    { translateY: activeTextBaseOffsetY },
   942	                  ],
   943	                },
   944	              ]}
   945	            >
   946	              {activeLink}
   947	            </Text>
   948	          </Pressable>
   949	
   950	          <Pressable
   951	            onPress={goToNextPage}
   952	            onPressIn={showHeldArrows}
   953	            onPressOut={hideHeldArrows}
   954	          >
   955	            <Animated.View
   956	              style={[
   957	                styles.arrowBox,
   958	                {
   959	                  opacity: rightArrowOpacity,
   960	                  transform: [
   961	                    { translateX: arrowLinkTranslateX },
   962	                  ],
   963	                },
   964	              ]}
   965	            >
   966	              {renderArrowChevron("right")}
   967	            </Animated.View>
   968	          </Pressable>
   969	        </Animated.View>
   970	
   971	        <Animated.View
   972	          pointerEvents="none"
   973	          style={[
   974	            styles.carouselIndicatorSeparator,
   975	            {
   976	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
   977	            },
   978	          ]}
   979	        />
   980	
   981	        <Animated.View
   982	          pointerEvents="none"
   983	          style={[
   984	            styles.carouselIndicatorBar,
   985	            {
   986	              transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
   987	            },
   988	          ]}
   989	        >
   990	          <View style={styles.carouselIndicatorTrack}>
   991	            {navPages.map((pageName) => (
   992	              <View
   993	                key={`carousel-indicator-track-${pageName}`}
   994	                style={styles.carouselIndicatorSegment}
   995	              />
   996	            ))}
   997	          </View>
   998	
   999	          <Animated.View
  1000	            style={[
  1001	              styles.carouselIndicatorActiveSegment,
  1002	              {
  1003	                width: indicatorSegmentWidth,
  1004	                transform: [{ translateX: indicatorTranslateX }],
  1005	              },
  1006	            ]}
  1007	          />
  1008	        </Animated.View>
  1009	      </Animated.View>
  1010	
  1011	    </View>
  1012	  );
  1013	
  1014	  const centerShadow = (
  1015	    <Animated.View
  1016	      pointerEvents="none"
  1017	      style={[
  1018	        styles.headerCenterShadow,
  1019	        {
  1020	          opacity: centerShadowOpacity,
  1021	          transform: [{ translateY: stickyOffset }],
  1022	        },
  1023	      ]}
  1024	    >
  1025	      {Platform.OS !== "web" && (
  1026	        <>
  1027	          {Array.from({ length: 36 }, (_, index) => (
  1028	            <View
  1029	              key={`center-shadow-layer-${index + 1}`}
  1030	              style={styles[`centerShadowLayer${index + 1}`]}
  1031	            />
  1032	          ))}
  1033	        </>
  1034	      )}
  1035	    </Animated.View>
  1036	  );
  1037	
  1038	  const hero = (
  1039	    <View
  1040	      style={styles.hero}
  1041	      {...carouselPanResponder.panHandlers}
  1042	      {...sharedHeaderTouchHandlers}
  1043	    >
  1044	      <Animated.Image
  1045	        source={heroImageSource}
  1046	        style={[
  1047	          isMainHeroPage ? styles.heroImageWidthFit : styles.heroImage,
  1048	          heroImageFrameStyle,
  1049	          heroImageTransformStyle,
  1050	          heroImageOpacityStyle,
  1051	        ]}
  1052	        resizeMode={isMainHeroPage ? "contain" : "cover"}
  1053	      />
  1054	      {isMainHeroPage
  1055	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1056	            "cheeseboard-current",
  1057	            cheeseboardBaseTranslateX,
  1058	            cheeseboardBaseOpacity
  1059	          )
  1060	        : null}
  1061	      {isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
  1062	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1063	            "cheeseboard-outgoing",
  1064	            cheeseboardOutgoingTranslateX
  1065	          )
  1066	        : null}
  1067	      {shouldShowCheeseboardDragPreviews ? (
  1068	        <>
  1069	          {renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1070	            "cheeseboard-previous-preview",
  1071	            cheeseboardPreviousPreviewTranslateX
  1072	          )}
  1073	          {renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1074	            "cheeseboard-next-preview",
  1075	            cheeseboardNextPreviewTranslateX
  1076	          )}
  1077	        </>
  1078	      ) : null}
  1079	      {isCheeseboardRouteTransitionActive
  1080	        ? renderCheeseboardLayer(
  1081	            "cheeseboard-incoming",
  1082	            cheeseboardIncomingTranslateX,
  1083	            cheeseboardIncomingOpacity
  1084	          )
  1085	        : null}
  1086	      {!isMainHeroPage ? (
  1087	        <Animated.View
  1088	          pointerEvents="none"
  1089	          style={[
  1090	            styles.heroVerticalFadePanel,
  1091	            {
  1092	              transform: [{ translateY: heroVerticalFadeTranslateY }],
  1093	            },
  1094	          ]}
  1095	        >
  1096	          <LinearGradient
  1097	            colors={["rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)", heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor]}
  1098	            locations={[0, 1]}
  1099	            style={styles.heroVerticalFadeFeather}
  1100	          />
  1101	          <View
  1102	            style={[
  1103	              styles.heroVerticalFadeSolid,
  1104	              { backgroundColor: heroVerticalFadeOverlayColor },
  1105	            ]}
  1106	          />
  1107	        </Animated.View>
  1108	      ) : null}
  1109	    </View>
  1110	  );
  1111	
  1112	  const heroOnlyLayer = (
  1113	    <Animated.View
  1114	      style={[
  1115	        styles.header,
  1116	        androidHeaderTopOverlapStyle,
  1117	        {
  1118	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
  1119	        },
  1120	      ]}
  1121	    >
  1122	      {centerShadow}
  1123	      <View style={styles.heroOnlySpacer} />
  1124	      {Platform.OS === "ios" ? (
  1125	        <Animated.View
  1126	          style={{
  1127	            transform: [{ translateY: carouselExpansionOffsetY }],
  1128	          }}
  1129	        >
  1130	          {hero}
  1131	        </Animated.View>
  1132	      ) : (
  1133	        hero
  1134	      )}
  1135	    </Animated.View>
  1136	  );
  1137	
  1138	  if (showOnlyCarousel) return carousel;
  1139	  if (showOnlyHero) return heroOnlyLayer;
  1140	
  1141	  return (
  1142	    <Animated.View
  1143	      style={[
  1144	        styles.header,
  1145	        androidHeaderTopOverlapStyle,
  1146	        {
  1147	          transform: [{ translateY: headerTranslateY }],
  1148	        },
  1149	      ]}
  1150	    >
  1151	      <Animated.View style={styles.orangeBar}>
  1152	        <Pressable style={styles.logoPressable} onPress={() => goToPage("home")}>
  1153	          <Text
  1154	            adjustsFontSizeToFit
  1155	            minimumFontScale={0.78}
  1156	            numberOfLines={1}
  1157	            style={styles.logoText}
  1158	          >
  1159	            Alla Vostra
  1160	          </Text>
  1161	        </Pressable>
  1162	
  1163	        {dimHeaderExceptShopButton ? (
  1164	          <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.orangeBarDimOverlay} />
  1165	        ) : null}
  1166	
  1167	        <Pressable
  1168	          style={[
  1169	            styles.shopButtonWrap,
  1170	            dimHeaderExceptShopButton && styles.shopButtonWrapSpotlight,
  1171	          ]}
  1172	          onPress={() => goToPage("shop")}
  1173	        >
  1174	          <ButtonShadowPlate style={styles.shopButtonShadowPlate} />
  1175	          <View style={styles.shopButton}>
  1176	            <Text style={styles.shopButtonText}>SHOP</Text>
  1177	          </View>
  1178	        </Pressable>
  1179	
  1180	        <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.orangeBarBottomHairline} />
  1181	      </Animated.View>
  1182	
  1183	      {showCarousel && showHero && centerShadow}
  1184	      {showCarousel && carousel}
  1185	      {showHero && hero}
  1186	    </Animated.View>
  1187	  );
  1188	}

FILE: mob/components/PageDivider.js
============================================================

     1	import { Animated } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import sharedStyles from "../styles/sharedStyles";
     4	
     5	const topDividerFadeScrollDistance = 240;
     6	
     7	export default function PageDivider({
     8	  expandedSpacing = false,
     9	  fadeScrollDistance = topDividerFadeScrollDistance,
    10	  fadeWithScrollY = null,
    11	}) {
    12	  const fadeOpacity = fadeWithScrollY?.interpolate({
    13	    inputRange: [0, fadeScrollDistance],
    14	    outputRange: [1, 0],
    15	    extrapolate: "clamp",
    16	  });
    17	
    18	  return (
    19	    <Animated.View
    20	      style={[
    21	        sharedStyles.pageDivider,
    22	        expandedSpacing && sharedStyles.expandedPageDivider,
    23	        fadeOpacity && { opacity: fadeOpacity },
    24	      ]}
    25	    />
    26	  );
    27	}

     1	import { LinearGradient } from "expo-linear-gradient";
     2	import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
     3	
     4	export default function ScreenFade({ showTopFade = true, topOffset = 84 }) {
     5	  return (
     6	    <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.overlay}>
     7	      {showTopFade ? (
     8	        <LinearGradient
     9	          pointerEvents="none"
    10	          colors={[
    11	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.24)",
    12	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.14)",
    13	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.045)",
    14	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.008)",
    15	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.001)",
    16	            "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
    17	          ]}
    18	          locations={[0, 0.14, 0.32, 0.52, 0.72, 1]}
    19	          style={[styles.topFade, { top: topOffset }]}
    20	        />
    21	      ) : null}
    22	
    23	      <LinearGradient
    24	        pointerEvents="none"
    25	        colors={[
    26	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0)",
    27	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.001)",
    28	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.008)",
    29	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.045)",
    30	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.14)",
    31	          "rgba(255, 252, 242, 0.24)",
    32	        ]}
    33	        locations={[0, 0.28, 0.48, 0.68, 0.86, 1]}
    34	        style={styles.bottomFade}
    35	      />
    36	    </View>
    37	  );
    38	}
    39	
    40	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
    41	  overlay: {
    42	    position: "absolute",
    43	    top: 0,
    44	    left: 0,
    45	    right: 0,
    46	    bottom: 0,
    47	    zIndex: 500000,
    48	    elevation: 500000,
    49	  },
    50	
    51	  topFade: {
    52	    position: "absolute",
    53	    left: 0,
    54	    right: 0,
    55	    height: 260,
    56	  },
    57	
    58	  bottomFade: {
    59	    position: "absolute",
    60	    left: 0,
    61	    right: 0,
    62	    bottom: 0,
    63	    height: 150,
    64	  },
    65	});

FILE: mob/styles/sharedStyles.js

     1	import { Platform, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import { bodyFont, tightText } from "./typography";
     4	import {
     5	  mainHorizontalPadding,
     6	  mainMaxWidth,
     7	  mainScreenContentTopInsetBase,
     8	  mainScreenInnerTopPadding,
     9	  mainScreenPageTitleHeight,
    10	  mainScreenPageTitleMarginBottom,

    13	  scaleVerticalGap,

FILE: mob/styles/shopStyles.js
============================================================

     1	import { Platform, StyleSheet } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	import {
     4	  heavyBlackBorderWithShadow,
     5	  tappableButtonShadowPlate,
     6	  thickBlackBorder,
     7	  thickBlackBorderShadow,
     8	  thickBlackBorderWithShadow,
     9	} from "./borderEffects";
    10	import {
    11	  bodyDemiBoldFont,
    12	  bodyFont,
    13	  bodyLightFont,
    14	  logoFont,
    15	  tightText,
    16	} from "./typography";
    17	import {
    18	  mainHorizontalPadding,
    19	  mainMaxWidth,
    20	  scaleLayout,
    21	  scaleVerticalGap,
    22	  isSmallAndroidViewport,
    23	  smallAndroidCreamAreaScale,
    24	} from "../utils/responsiveLayout";
    25	import {
    26	  shopOverlayActionToStickyButtonRatio,
    27	  stickyButtonEdgeOffset,
    28	  stickyButtonRadius,
    29	  stickyButtonSize,
    30	} from "../utils/stickyButtonLayout";
    31	
    32	const shippingPreviewIOSLayoutScale = 0.77;
    33	const scaleShippingPreview = (value) =>
    34	  Platform.select({
    35	    ios: value * shippingPreviewIOSLayoutScale,
    36	    default: scaleVerticalGap(value) * smallAndroidCreamAreaScale,
    37	  });
    38	const productOverlayIOSScale = 0.82;
    39	const scaleProductOverlay = (value) =>
    40	  Platform.select({
    41	    ios: value * productOverlayIOSScale,
    42	    default: value * smallAndroidCreamAreaScale,
    43	  });
    44	const cartOverlayFilledIOSScale = 0.72;
    45	const scaleCartOverlayFilled = (value) =>
    46	  Platform.select({
    47	    ios: value * cartOverlayFilledIOSScale,
    48	    default: value * smallAndroidCreamAreaScale,
    49	  });
    50	const cartOverlayAddedProductAssetScale = 1.215;
    51	const scaleCartOverlayAddedProduct = (value) =>
    52	  scaleCartOverlayFilled(value * cartOverlayAddedProductAssetScale);
    53	const cartOverlayReceiptIOSScale = 0.78;
    54	const scaleCartOverlayReceipt = (value) =>
    55	  Platform.select({
    56	    ios: value * cartOverlayReceiptIOSScale,
    57	    default: value * smallAndroidCreamAreaScale,
    58	  });
    59	const scaleCartOverlayGrandTotal = (value) =>
    60	  Platform.select({
    61	    ios: value * 0.68,
    62	    default: value * smallAndroidCreamAreaScale,
    63	  });
    64	const scaleCartOverlayCheckoutBox = (value) =>
    65	  Platform.select({
    66	    ios: value * 0.78,
    67	    default: value * smallAndroidCreamAreaScale,
    68	  });
    69	const isAndroidPlatform = Platform.OS === "android";
    70	const isSmallAndroidPlatform = isAndroidPlatform && isSmallAndroidViewport;
    71	const overlayActionButtonHeight = isAndroidPlatform
    72	  ? stickyButtonSize * shopOverlayActionToStickyButtonRatio
    73	  : scaleCartOverlayCheckoutBox(55.5);
    74	const overlayActionButtonWidth = isAndroidPlatform
    75	  ? overlayActionButtonHeight * 2
    76	  : scaleCartOverlayCheckoutBox(111);
    77	const overlayActionButtonRadius = isAndroidPlatform
    78	  ? stickyButtonRadius * shopOverlayActionToStickyButtonRatio
    79	  : scaleCartOverlayCheckoutBox(10.5);
    80	const overlayActionButtonTextFontSize = isAndroidPlatform
    81	  ? overlayActionButtonHeight * (15.84 / 55.5)
    82	  : scaleCartOverlayGrandTotal(15.84);
    83	const overlayProductActionButtonSize = isAndroidPlatform
    84	  ? overlayActionButtonHeight
    85	  : scaleProductOverlay(55.5);
    86	const overlayProductActionButtonRadius = isAndroidPlatform
    87	  ? overlayActionButtonRadius
    88	  : scaleProductOverlay(10.5);
    89	const overlayProductActionButtonTextFontSize = isAndroidPlatform

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/headerScrollContext.js
============================================================

     1	import { createContext, useContext } from "react";
     2	
     3	const HeaderScrollContext = createContext(null);
     4	
     5	export function HeaderScrollProvider({ children, scrollY }) {
     6	  return (
     7	    <HeaderScrollContext.Provider value={scrollY}>
     8	      {children}
     9	    </HeaderScrollContext.Provider>
    10	  );
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function useHeaderScrollY() {
    14	  return useContext(HeaderScrollContext);
    15	}

============================================================
FILE: mob/utils/openPaymentLink.js
============================================================

     1	import { Alert, Linking, Platform } from "react-native";
     2	
     3	export async function openPaymentLink(url) {
     4	  if (!url) {
     5	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
     6	      window.alert("No payment link has been added for this product yet.");
     7	      return;
     8	    }
     9	
    10	    Alert.alert("Payment link missing", "No payment link has been added yet.");
    11	    return;
    12	  }
    13	
    14	  try {
    15	    const canOpen = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
    16	
    17	    if (!canOpen) {
    18	      throw new Error("Cannot open URL");
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    await Linking.openURL(url);
    22	  } catch (error) {
    23	    if (Platform.OS === "web") {
    24	      window.alert("Unable to open this payment link.");
    25	      return;
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    Alert.alert("Unable to open link", "Please try again later.");
    29	  }
    30	}
